We found this same pattern when using high-dimensional embeddings from both self-supervised visual and multimodal models; this effect was also recapitulated in densely sampled data from individual children. Understanding the robustness and efficiency of visual category learning will require the development of models that can exploit strong superordinate structure and learn from non-canonical, sparse, and variable exemplars.

中文摘要翻译: 儿童在生命最初几年通过日常经验获取物体类别表征。这些学习过程的输入是什么样的？我们利用BabyView数据集（N=31名参与者，868小时，年龄5-36个月）中儿童家庭视觉经验的第一人称视频，使用监督目标检测模型从超过300万帧中提取常见物体类别。我们发现，儿童物体类别暴露高度偏斜：少数类别（如杯子、椅子）主导了儿童的视觉经验，而大多数类别很少出现，这复现了先前在更有限背景下的研究结果。类别样本高度可变：儿童从不寻常角度、高度杂乱场景和部分遮挡视角中遇到物体；许多类别（特别是动物）最常见的形式是图像呈现。令人惊讶的是，尽管存在这种变异性，检测到的类别（如长颈鹿、苹果）在上位类别（如动物、食物）内的分组比从这些类别的规范照片中导出的分组更强。我们在自监督视觉和多模态模型的高维嵌入中也发现了相同的模式；这一效应在个体儿童的密集采样数据中也被重现。理解视觉类别学习的鲁棒性和效率将需要开发能够利用强上位类别结构并从非典型、稀疏和可变的样本中学习的模型。

</details>


### [90] [Predicting Response to Neoadjuvant Chemotherapy in Ovarian Cancer from CT Baseline Using Multi-Loss Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14991)
*Francesco Pastori,Francesca Fati,Marina Rosanu,Luigi De Vitis,Lucia Ribero,Gabriella Schivardi,Giovanni Damiano Aletti,Nicoletta Colombo,Jvan Casarin,Francesco Multinu,Elena De Momi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种非侵入性深度学习框架，利用治疗前增强CT的3D病灶掩膜预测卵巢癌患者对新辅助化疗的反应，在测试集上达到0.73的ROC-AUC和0.70的F1分数。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 卵巢癌是最致命的妇科恶性肿瘤，约60%患者确诊时已处于晚期，5年生存率仅约30%。早期识别新辅助化疗无应答者是一个关键未满足的临床需求，可以避免无效治疗并防止延误最佳手术时机。

Method: 提出一种非侵入性深度学习框架，利用治疗前增强CT自动提取的3D病灶掩膜。使用部分微调的预训练图像编码器编码轴向切片，通过基于注意力的模块将切片级表示聚合为三维嵌入。训练结合分类损失、有监督对比正则化和难负样本挖掘，以改善应答者与无应答者之间的区分。

Result: 在米兰欧洲肿瘤研究所280例患者（147例应答者，133例无应答者）的单中心回顾性队列上开发，测试集上达到ROC-AUC 0.73（95% CI: 0.58-0.86）和F1分数0.70（95% CI: 0.56-0.82）。

Conclusion: 所提出的架构能够学习临床相关的预测模式，为基于影像的分层工具提供了坚实的 foundation。

Original Abstract: Ovarian cancer is the most lethal gynecologic malignancy: around 60% of patients are diagnosed at an advanced stage, with an associated 5-year survival rate of about 30%. Early identification of non-responders to neoadjuvant chemotherapy remains a key unmet need, as it could prevent ineffective therapy and avoid delays in optimal surgical management. This work proposes a non-invasive deep learning framework to predict neoadjuvant chemotherapy response from pre-treatment contrast-enhanced CT by leveraging automatically derived 3D lesion masks. The approach encodes axial slices with a partially fine-tuned pretrained image encoder and aggregates slice-level representations into a volumetric embedding through an attention-based module. Training combines classification loss with supervised contrastive regularization and hard-negative mining to improve separation between ambiguous responders and non-responders. The method was developed on a retrospective single-center cohort from the European Institute of Oncology (Milan, IT), including 280 eligible patients (147 responder, 133 non-responder). On the test cohort, the model achieved a ROC-AUC of 0.73 (95% CI: 0.58-0.86) and an F1-score of 0.70 (95% CI: 0.56-0.82). Overall, these results suggest that the proposed architecture learns clinically relevant predictive patterns and provides a robust foundation for an imaging-based stratification tool.

中文摘要翻译: 卵巢癌是最致命的妇科恶性肿瘤：约60%的患者在确诊时已处于晚期，其5年生存率约为30%。早期识别新辅助化疗的无应答者仍是一个关键未满足的临床需求，因为这可避免无效治疗并防止延误最佳手术时机。本文提出了一种非侵入性深度学习框架，通过利用自动推导的3D病灶掩膜，从治疗前增强CT预测新辅助化疗反应。该方法使用部分微调的预训练图像编码器对轴向切片进行编码，并通过基于注意力的模块将切片级表示聚合为三维嵌入。训练过程结合了分类损失、有监督对比正则化和难负样本挖掘，以改善模棱两可的应答者与无应答者之间的分离。该方法在米兰欧洲肿瘤研究所的单中心回顾性队列上开发，包括280例符合条件的患者（147例应答者，133例无应答者）。在测试队列上，该模型达到了0.73的ROC-AUC（95%置信区间：0.58-0.86）和0.70的F1分数（95%置信区间：0.56-0.82）。总体而言，这些结果表明所提出的架构学习了临床相关的预测模式，并为基于影像的分层工具提供了坚实的 foundation。

</details>


### [91] [3D Skew-Normal Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15010)
*Xiangru Wu,Ke Fan,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出偏态正态样条（SNS），采用Azzalini偏态正态分布作为高斯样条的基础原语，通过引入可学习的偏态参数实现从对称高斯到半高斯形状的连续插值，在有限原语预算下更紧凑地表示非对称结构（如物体边界和单侧表面），并在新视角合成基准测试中持续提升了重建质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的3D高斯样条（3DGS）在有限原语预算下，每个原语的对称形状直接影响了表示的紧凑性，尤其在非对称结构（如物体边界和单侧表面）附近表现不佳。已有的改进工作要么仍局限于椭圆族分布，要么依赖硬截断导致连续形状控制受限和分布不连续性。因此，需要一种能够灵活建模对称与非对称结构且保持解析可处理性的新原语分布。

Method: 本文提出Skew-Normal Splatting（SNS），采用Azzalini偏态正态分布作为基础原语，引入可学习且有界的偏态参数，实现对称高斯与半高斯形状之间的连续插值。SNS保持仿射变换和边缘化下的解析可处理性，可无缝集成到现有高斯样条光栅化流水线中。针对尺度、旋转和偏态参数之间的强耦合问题，提出解耦参数化和分块优化策略以提升训练稳定性与精度。

Result: 在新视角合成标准基准上的大量实验表明，SNS在重建质量上持续优于高斯和最近的非高斯核方法，在锐利边界、薄结构和单侧结构上具有更明显的优势。

Conclusion: 偏态正态分布作为一种灵活且解析可处理的基础原语，能够有效弥补传统对称高斯原语在表示非对称结构时的不足，为3DGS框架下的场景表示提供了更紧凑、更精确的方案。

Original Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a leading representation for real-time novel view synthesis and been widely adopted in various downstream applications. The core strength of 3DGS lies in its efficient kernel-based scene representation, where Gaussian primitives provide favorable mathematical and computational properties. However, under a finite primitive budget, the symmetric shape of each primitive directly affects representation compactness, especially near asymmetric structures such as object boundaries and one-sided surfaces. Recent works have explored more complex kernel distributions, yet they either remain within the elliptical family or rely on hard truncation, which limits continuous shape control and introduces distributional discontinuities. In this paper, we propose Skew-Normal Splatting (SNS), which adopts the Azzalini Skew-Normal distribution as the fundamental primitive. By introducing a learnable and bounded skewness parameter, SNS can continuously interpolate between symmetric Gaussians and Half-Gaussian-like shapes, enabling flexible modeling of both sharp boundaries and interior regions. Moremover, SNS preserves analytical tractability under affine transformations and marginalization. This property allows seamless integration into existing Gaussian Splatting rasterization pipelines.Furthermore, to address the strong coupling between scale, rotation, and skewness parameters, we introduce a decoupled parameterization and a block-wise optimization strategy to enhance training stability and accuracy. Extensive experiments on standard novel-view synthesis benchmarks show that SNS consistently improves reconstruction quality over Gaussian and recent non-Gaussian kernels, with clearer benefits on sharp boundaries and thin or one-sided structures.

中文摘要翻译: 3D高斯样条（3DGS）已成为实时新视角合成的领先表示方法，并被广泛用于各类下游应用。3DGS的核心优势在于其高效的基于核的场景表示，其中高斯原语具有优越的数学和计算性质。然而，在有限的原语预算下，每个原语的对称形状直接影响表示的紧凑性，尤其在非对称结构（如物体边界和单侧表面）附近表现明显。最近的工作探索了更复杂的核分布，但它们要么仍局限于椭圆族分布，要么依赖硬截断，这限制了连续的形状控制并引入了分布不连续性。本文提出偏态正态样条（Skew-Normal Splatting, SNS），采用Azzalini偏态正态分布作为基础原语。通过引入可学习且有界的偏态参数，SNS可以在对称高斯和半高斯形状之间连续插值，从而灵活建模锐利边界和内部区域。此外，SNS在仿射变换和边缘化下保持解析可处理性，这一性质使其能够无缝集成到现有的高斯样条光栅化流水线中。进一步地，针对尺度、旋转和偏态参数之间的强耦合问题，我们引入了解耦参数化和分块优化策略，以提升训练的稳定性和精度。在新视角合成标准基准上的大量实验表明，SNS在重建质量上持续优于高斯和最近的非高斯核方法，在锐利边界以及薄或单侧结构上具有更明显的优势。

</details>


### [92] [EverAnimate: Minute-Scale Human Animation via Latent Flow Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15042)
*Wuyang Li,Yang Gao,Mariam Hassan,Lan Feng,Wentao Pan,Po-Chien Luan,Alexandre Alahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EverAnimate是一种高效的后训练方法，通过持久潜在上下文记忆（包含持久潜在传播和恢复性流匹配）来解决长视频动画生成中的累积漂移问题，仅用轻量LoRA微调即在10秒和90秒设置下显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 长视频动画生成中，高度动态的人体运动需在相对静态的环境中合成，导致基于分块的生成出现累积漂移：低层质量漂移（如静态背景逐步退化）和高层语义漂移（如角色身份和视角相关属性不一致）。现有方法难以同时解决这两类漂移。

Method: 提出EverAnimate，包含两个互补机制：(i) 持久潜在传播（Persistent Latent Propagation）：跨分块维护上下文记忆，在潜在空间中传播身份和运动信息，缓解时间遗忘；(ii) 恢复性流匹配（Restorative Flow Matching）：在采样过程中通过速度调整引入隐式恢复目标，提升分块内保真度。仅需轻量LoRA微调。

Result: 在10秒设置下，PSNR/SSIM提升8%/7%，LPIPS/FID降低22%/11%；在90秒设置下，PSNR/SSIM提升15%/15%，LPIPS/FID降低32%/27%。

Conclusion: EverAnimate通过持久潜在上下文记忆有效解决了长视频动画中的累积漂移问题，在长短时间跨度设置下均达到最优性能，且仅需轻量微调即可实现显著提升。

Original Abstract: We propose EverAnimate, an efficient post-training method for long-horizon animated video generation that preserves visual quality and character identity. Long-form animation remains challenging because highly dynamic human motion must be synthesized against relatively static environments, making chunk-based generation prone to accumulated drift: (i) low-level quality drift, such as progressive degradation of static backgrounds, and (ii) high-level semantic drift, such as inconsistent character identity and view-dependent attributes. To address this issue, EverAnimate restores drifted flow trajectories by anchoring generation to a persistent latent context memory, consisting of two complementary mechanisms. (i) Persistent Latent Propagation maintains a context memory across chunks to propagate identity and motion in latent space while mitigating temporal forgetting. (ii) Restorative Flow Matching introduces an implicit restoration objective during sampling through velocity adjustment, improving within-chunk fidelity. With only lightweight LoRA tuning, EverAnimate outperforms state-of-the-art long-animation methods in both short- and long-horizon settings: at 10 seconds, it improves PSNR/SSIM by 8%/7% and reduces LPIPS/FID by 22%/11%; at 90 seconds, the gains increase to 15%/15% and 32%/27%, respectively.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了EverAnimate，一种用于长时序动画视频生成的高效后训练方法，能够在保持视觉质量和角色身份的同时生成动画。长视频动画仍然具有挑战性，因为高度动态的人体运动必须在相对静态的环境中合成，这使得基于分块的生成容易产生累积漂移：(i) 低层质量漂移，例如静态背景的逐步退化，以及(ii) 高层语义漂移，例如角色身份和视角相关属性的不一致。为了解决这一问题，EverAnimate通过将生成锚定到持久潜在上下文记忆来恢复漂移的流轨迹，该记忆由两种互补机制组成。(i) 持久潜在传播（Persistent Latent Propagation）跨分块维护上下文记忆，在潜在空间中传播身份和运动信息，同时缓解时间遗忘。(ii) 恢复性流匹配（Restorative Flow Matching）在采样过程中通过速度调整引入隐式恢复目标，提升分块内保真度。仅通过轻量级的LoRA微调，EverAnimate在短时序和长时序设置下均优于最先进的长视频动画方法：在10秒设置下，PSNR/SSIM提升8%/7%，LPIPS/FID降低22%/11%；在90秒设置下，提升幅度分别增至15%/15%和32%/27%。

</details>


### [93] [LATERN: Test-Time Context-Aware Explainable Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15054)
*Mitchell Piehl,Muchao Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LATERN是一个上下文感知的视频异常检测框架，通过时间证据聚合机制，使冻结的视觉-语言模型能够生成时间连贯且语义可解释的事件级异常检测结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于视觉-语言模型的视频异常检测方法因token限制而独立地进行片段级推理，缺乏结构化时间上下文，导致预测和解释碎片化。本文旨在让VLMs能够将异常理解为偏离动态演化的视频上下文的偏差，而非产生孤立的预测。

Method: 提出LATERN框架，包含两个互补模块：(1) 上下文感知异常评分（CEA），通过图像引导的记忆机制，利用帧多样性和视觉-文本对齐选择性选取历史内容作为扩展上下文，生成可靠异常分数；(2) 递归证据聚合（REA），基于CEA分数进行递归时间聚合，识别连贯的异常区间，生成基于视觉-文本证据的事件级决策和解释。

Result: 在UCF-Crime和XD-Violence等挑战性基准上，LATERN提升了冻结VLMs在测试时的检测精度和解释一致性，生成了时间连贯且语义可解释的事件级解释。

Conclusion: LATERN通过将VAD重新定义为时间证据聚合过程，有效解决了VLMs在视频异常检测中缺乏时间上下文的问题，在无需微调的情况下显著提升了检测性能和解释质量。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for video anomaly detection (VAD) due to their strong visual reasoning ability and natural language-based explainability. In this paper, we aim to address a key limitation of such pipelines, which perform segment-level inference independently owing to token constraints and reason without structured temporal context, allowing VLMs to interpret anomalies as deviations from evolving video dynamics rather than producing fragmented predictions and explanations. To specify, we propose a context-aware framework named LATERN, which reformulates VAD as a temporal evidence aggregation process. LATERN consists of two complementary modules: Context-Aware Anomaly Scoring (CEA) and Recursive Evidence Aggregation (REA). CEA introduces a novel image-grounded memory mechanism, which selectively chooses historical content via frame diversity and visual-textual alignment as expanded context to help generate reliable anomaly scores. Building upon these scores, REA performs recursive temporal aggregation to identify coherent anomaly intervals and produce event-level decisions and explanations grounded in visual-textual evidence. Extensive experiments on challenging benchmarks, including UCF-Crime and XD-Violence, show that LATERN enhances detection accuracy and explanation consistency for frozen VLMs during test time, while generating temporally coherent and semantically grounded event-level explanations.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）因其强大的视觉推理能力和基于自然语言的可解释性，最近成为视频异常检测（VAD）的一种有前景的范式。在本文中，我们旨在解决此类流程的一个关键局限性：由于token约束，它们独立地进行片段级推理，并且推理时缺乏结构化的时间上下文。我们让VLMs能够将异常解释为偏离动态演化的视频上下文的偏差，而非产生碎片化的预测和解释。具体来说，我们提出了一个名为LATERN的上下文感知框架，它将VAD重新定义为时间证据聚合过程。LATERN由两个互补模块组成：上下文感知异常评分（CEA）和递归证据聚合（REA）。CEA引入了一种新颖的图像引导记忆机制，通过帧多样性和视觉-文本对齐选择性选取历史内容作为扩展上下文，以帮助生成可靠的异常分数。基于这些分数，REA执行递归时间聚合以识别连贯的异常区间，并生成基于视觉-文本证据的事件级决策和解释。在包括UCF-Crime和XD-Violence在内的挑战性基准上的大量实验表明，LATERN在测试时提升了冻结VLMs的检测精度和解释一致性，同时生成了时间连贯且语义可解释的事件级解释。

</details>


### [94] [On the Cultural Anachronism and Temporal Reasoning in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15071)
*Mukul Ranjan,Prince Jha,Khushboo Kumari,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉语言模型在解释历史文物时存在将现代概念错置于古代语境的现象（称为"文化时代错位"），并构建了TAB-VLM基准数据集来量化该问题。评估10个SOTA模型后发现最佳模型GPT-5.2仅达58.7%准确率，表明模型规模无法弥补这一缺陷，尤其对非西方视觉文化表现更差。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在文化遗产材料（如数字档案、教育平台）中广泛应用，但本文发现它们存在系统性地使用时代不当的概念、材料或文化框架来误解历史文物的问题，现有基准缺乏对此类时间推理缺陷的评估。

Method: 定义了"文化时代错位"现象，构建了TAB-VLM基准数据集，包含600个问题、覆盖6个类别、涉及1,600件印度文化文物（从史前到现代）。系统评估了10个最先进的视觉语言模型。

Result: 最佳模型GPT-5.2仅达到58.7%的整体准确率；性能差距在不同架构和模型规模下持续存在；非西方视觉文化（训练数据中代表性不足）的表现更差。

Conclusion: 文化时代错位是视觉AI系统的显著局限，不受模型规模影响；现有VLM能力与准确解读文化遗产材料的要求之间存在显著差距；该基准为增强多模态AI系统的时间认知能力提供了基础。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly applied to cultural heritage materials, from digital archives to educational platforms. This work identifies a fundamental issue in how these models interpret historical artifacts. We define this phenomenon as cultural anachronism, the tendency to misinterpret historical objects using temporally inappropriate concepts, materials, or cultural frameworks. To quantify this phenomenon, we introduce the Temporal Anachronism Benchmark for Vision-Language Models (TAB-VLM), a dataset of 600 questions across six categories, designed to evaluate temporal reasoning on 1,600 Indian cultural artifacts spanning prehistoric to modern periods. Systematic evaluations of ten state-of-the-art models reveal significant deficiencies on our benchmark, and even the best model (GPT-5.2) achieves only 58.7% overall accuracy. The performance gap persists across varying architectures and scales, suggesting that cultural anachronism represents a significant limitation in visual AI systems, regardless of model size. These findings highlight the disparity between current VLM capabilities and the requirements for accurately interpreting cultural heritage materials, particularly for non-Western visual cultures underrepresented in training data. Our benchmark provides a foundation for enhancing temporal cognition in multimodal AI systems that interact with historical artifacts. The dataset and code are available in our project page.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉语言模型（VLM）越来越多地被应用于文化遗产材料，从数字档案到教育平台。本文识别了这些模型在解释历史文物时的一个根本性问题。我们将这一现象定义为"文化时代错位"（cultural anachronism），即使用时代不当的概念、材料或文化框架来误解历史对象的倾向。为量化这一现象，我们引入了面向视觉语言模型的时间错位基准（Temporal Anachronism Benchmark for Vision-Language Models, TAB-VLM），这是一个包含600个问题、覆盖6个类别的数据集，旨在评估对1,600件印度文化文物（涵盖从史前到现代的各个时期）的时间推理能力。对十个最先进模型的系统评估揭示了它们在该基准上的显著缺陷，即使是最佳模型（GPT-5.2）也仅达到58.7%的整体准确率。这一性能差距在不同架构和模型规模下持续存在，表明文化时代错位是视觉AI系统的一个显著局限，且不受模型大小影响。这些发现凸显了当前VLM能力与准确解读文化遗产材料要求之间的差距，尤其是对于训练数据中代表性不足的非西方视觉文化。我们的基准为增强与历史文物交互的多模态AI系统的时间认知能力提供了基础。数据集和代码已发布于我们的项目页面。

</details>


### [95] [DriveCtrl: Conditioned Sim-to-Real Driving Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15116)
*Haonan Zhao,Yiting Wang,Jingkun Chen,Valentina Donzella,Thomas Bashford-Rogers,Kurt Debattista*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveCtrl 是一个深度条件控制的仿真到真实视频生成框架，通过结构感知适配器实现深度引导生成，缩小自动驾驶视频的仿真-真实域差距，并提出DVRS评估指标。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大规模标注的真实驾驶视频数据难以获取，仿真数据虽可扩展但存在严重的域差距限制了其在下游部署中的效用，现有视频生成方法无法同时保持场景结构、物体动态、时序一致性和视觉真实性。

Method: 基于预训练视频基础模型，引入结构感知适配器实现深度引导生成；构建可扩展的数据生成流水线，支持结构深度、参考数据集风格和文本提示三种条件信号；提出驾驶领域知识驱动的评估指标DVRS。

Result: DriveCtrl在真实性、时序质量和感知任务性能上持续优于基线和竞品方法，显著缩小了驾驶视频生成的仿真-真实域差距。

Conclusion: DriveCtrl通过深度条件的可控仿真到真实视频生成，有效保留了场景布局和运动模式，生成了与原始仿真序列对齐的时序连贯驾驶视频，为自动驾驶系统训练提供了更实用的数据解决方案。

Original Abstract: Large-scale labelled driving video data is essential for training autonomous driving systems. Although simulation offers scalable and fully annotated data, the domain gap between synthetic and real-world driving videos significantly limits its utility for downstream deployment. Existing video generation methods are not well-suited for this task, as they fail to simultaneously preserve scene structure, object dynamics, temporal consistency, and visual realism, all of which are critical for maintaining annotation validity in generated data. In this paper, we present DriveCtrl, a depth-conditioned controllable sim-to-real video generation framework for realistic driving video synthesis. Built upon a pretrained video foundation model, DriveCtrl introduces a structure-aware adapter that enables depth-guided generation while preserving the scene layout and motion patterns of the source simulation, producing temporally coherent driving videos that remain aligned with the original simulated sequences. We further introduce a scalable data generation pipeline that transforms simulator videos into realistic driving footage matching the visual style of a target real-world dataset. The pipeline supports three conditioning signals: structural depth, reference-dataset style, and text prompts, while preserving frame-level annotations for downstream perception tasks. To better assess this task, we propose a driving-domain-specific knowledge-informed evaluation metric called Driving Video Realism Score (DVRS) that assesses the realism of generated videos. Experiments demonstrate that DriveCtrl consistently outperforms the base model and competing alternatives in realism, temporal quality, and perception task performance, substantially narrowing the sim-to-real gap for driving video generation.

中文摘要翻译: 大规模标注的驾驶视频数据对于训练自动驾驶系统至关重要。尽管仿真可以提供可扩展且完全标注的数据，但合成驾驶视频与真实驾驶视频之间的域差距显著限制了其在下游部署中的效用。现有视频生成方法并不适用于此任务，因为它们无法同时保持场景结构、物体动态、时序一致性和视觉真实性，而这些对于保持生成数据中标注的有效性都至关重要。本文提出了DriveCtrl，一个深度条件可控的仿真到真实视频生成框架，用于合成逼真的驾驶视频。该框架基于预训练的视频基础模型，引入了一个结构感知适配器，在保留源仿真的场景布局和运动模式的同时实现深度引导生成，生成与原始仿真序列对齐的时序连贯驾驶视频。我们进一步引入了一个可扩展的数据生成流水线，将仿真器视频转换为与目标真实数据集视觉风格相匹配的逼真驾驶画面。该流水线支持三种条件信号：结构深度、参考数据集风格和文本提示，同时为下游感知任务保留帧级标注。为了更好地评估此任务，我们提出了一种驾驶领域知识驱动的评估指标，称为驾驶视频真实性评分（DVRS），用于评估生成视频的真实性。实验表明，DriveCtrl在真实性、时序质量和感知任务性能上持续优于基线和竞品方法，显著缩小了驾驶视频生成的仿真到真实域差距。

</details>


### [96] [Causal Forcing++: Scalable Few-Step Autoregressive Diffusion Distillation for Real-Time Interactive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15141)
*Min Zhao,Hongzhou Zhu,Kaiwen Zheng,Zihan Zhou,Bokai Yan,Xinyuan Li,Xiao Yang,Chongxuan Li,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Causal Forcing++，一种用于帧级自回归视频生成的高效初始化方法。通过因果一致性蒸馏（Causal CD），在1-2步采样下实现帧级视频生成，相比SOTA的4步块级方法，在多个指标上取得提升，同时将首帧延迟降低50%，训练成本减少约4倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自回归扩散蒸馏方法在块级4步生成上表现良好，但存在响应粒度粗、采样延迟高的问题。本文研究更激进的帧级1-2步自回归生成，发现少数步自回归学生的初始化是关键瓶颈：现有策略要么目标不对齐，要么无法进行少数步生成，要么成本过高难以扩展。

Method: 提出因果一致性蒸馏（Causal CD）用于帧级少数步自回归初始化。核心思想是：Causal CD学习与因果ODE蒸馏相同的自回归条件流映射，但仅从相邻时间步之间的单步在线教师ODE中获得监督，避免了预计算和存储完整PF-ODE轨迹的需求，使初始化更高效且更易优化。

Result: 在帧级2步设置下，Causal Forcing++超越SOTA的4步块级Causal Forcing：VBench Total提升0.1，VBench Quality提升0.3，VisionReward提升0.335；首帧延迟降低50%，Stage 2训练成本减少约4倍。

Conclusion: Causal Forcing++提供了一种原则性且可扩展的帧级少数步自回归视频生成方案，在降低延迟和训练成本的同时提升了生成质量，并可拓展至动作条件世界模型生成。

Original Abstract: Real-time interactive video generation requires low-latency, streaming, and controllable rollout. Existing autoregressive (AR) diffusion distillation methods have achieved strong results in the chunk-wise 4-step regime by distilling bidirectional base models into few-step AR students, but they remain limited by coarse response granularity and non-negligible sampling latency. In this paper, we study a more aggressive setting: frame-wise autoregression with only 1--2 sampling steps. In this regime, we identify the initialization of a few-step AR student as the key bottleneck: existing strategies are either target-misaligned, incapable of few-step generation, or too costly to scale. We propose \textbf{Causal Forcing++}, a principled and scalable pipeline that uses \emph{causal consistency distillation} (causal CD) for few-step AR initialization. The core idea is that causal CD learns the same AR-conditional flow map as causal ODE distillation, but obtains supervision from a single online teacher ODE step between adjacent timesteps, avoiding the need to precompute and store full PF-ODE trajectories. This makes the initialization both more efficient and easier to optimize. The resulting pipeline, \ours, surpasses the SOTA 4-step chunk-wise Causal Forcing under the \textit{\textbf{frame-wise 2-step setting}} by 0.1 in VBench Total, 0.3 in VBench Quality, and 0.335 in VisionReward, while reducing first-frame latency by 50\% and Stage 2 training cost by $\sim$$4\times$. We further extend the pipeline to action-conditioned world model generation in the spirit of Genie3. Project Page: https://github.com/thu-ml/Causal-Forcing and https://github.com/shengshu-ai/minWM .

中文摘要翻译: 实时交互式视频生成需要低延迟、流式处理和可控的推出机制。现有的自回归（AR）扩散蒸馏方法通过将双向基模型蒸馏为少数步AR学生，在块级4步生成中取得了强劲结果，但仍受限于粗糙的响应粒度和不可忽略的采样延迟。在本文中，我们研究了一个更激进的设定：仅需1-2步采样的帧级自回归生成。在该设定下，我们识别出少数步AR学生的初始化是关键瓶颈：现有策略要么目标不对齐，要么无法进行少数步生成，要么成本过高难以扩展。我们提出\textbf{Causal Forcing++}，一种原则性且可扩展的流水线，使用\textit{因果一致性蒸馏}（causal CD）进行少数步AR初始化。核心思想是：causal CD学习与因果ODE蒸馏相同的AR条件流映射，但仅从相邻时间步之间的单步在线教师ODE中获得监督，避免了预计算和存储完整PF-ODE轨迹的需求。这使得初始化更高效且更易优化。所得到的流水线\ours在\textit{\textbf{帧级2步设定}}下全面超越SOTA的4步块级Causal Forcing：VBench Total提升0.1，VBench Quality提升0.3，VisionReward提升0.335，同时将首帧延迟降低50%，Stage 2训练成本减少约\texttt{4}倍。我们进一步将流水线扩展至Genie3框架下的动作条件世界模型生成。项目页面：https://github.com/thu-ml/Causal-Forcing 和 https://github.com/shengshu-ai/minWM。

</details>


### [97] [Does Synthetic Layered Design Data Benefit Layered Design Decomposition?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15167)
*Kam Man Wu,Haolin Yang,Qingyu Chen,Yihu Tang,Jingye Chen,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨纯合成分层数据能否改进图形设计分解，通过构建SynLayers合成数据集，基于CLD基线进行数据为中心的研究，发现纯合成数据训练可超越真实数据集，性能随数据规模提升并在5万样本附近饱和，且合成数据能平衡图层数量分布。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像生成方法产生的是扁平化输出，前景、背景和文本纠缠在固定画布中，导致灵活的后生成编辑困难。已有方法要么依赖稀缺的专有分层资产，要么从有限结构先验构建部分合成数据，都面临可扩展性挑战。因此，作者探究纯合成分层数据能否改进图形设计分解。

Method: 基于CLD基线（最先进的图层分解框架），构建纯合成数据集SynLayers；使用视觉语言模型生成文本监督信号；利用VLM预测的边界框自动化推理输入。通过数据为中心的研究方法，系统考察纯合成数据对图形设计分解性能的影响。

Result: (1) 纯合成数据训练可超越非可扩展替代方案（如PrismLayersPro数据集），证明了其作为可扩展且有效替代方案的可行性；(2) 性能随训练数据规模一致提升，增益在5万样本左右趋于饱和；(3) 合成数据能实现图层数量分布的平衡控制，避免真实数据中常见的图层数量不平衡问题。

Conclusion: 纯合成分层数据可作为图形设计分解任务的可扩展且有效的替代方案，该数据为中心的研究鼓励更广泛地将合成数据作为分层设计编辑系统的实用基础。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in image generation have made it easy to produce high-quality images. However, these outputs are inherently flattened, entangling foreground elements, background, and text within a fixed canvas. As a result, flexible post-generation editing remains challenging, revealing a clear last-mile gap toward practical usability. Existing approaches either rely on scarce proprietary layered assets or construct partially synthetic data from limited structural priors. However, both strategies face fundamental challenges in scalability. In this work, we investigate whether pure synthetic layered data can improve graphic design decomposition. We make the assumption that, in graphic design, effective decomposition does not require modeling inter-layer dependencies as precisely as in natural-image composition, since design elements are often intentionally arranged as modular and semantically separable components. Concretely, we conduct a data-centric study based on CLD baseline, which is a state-of-the-art layer decomposition framework. Based on the baseline, we construct our own synthetic dataset, SynLayers, generate textual supervision using vision language models, and automate inference inputs with VLM-predicted bounding boxes. Our study reveals three key findings: (1) even training with purely synthetic data can outperform non-scalable alternatives such as the widely used PrismLayersPro dataset, demonstrating its viability as a scalable and effective substitute; (2) performance consistently improves with increased training data scale, while gains begin to saturate at around 50K samples; and (3) synthetic data enables balanced control over layer-count distributions, avoiding the layer-count imbalance commonly observed in real-world datasets. We hope this data-centric study encourages broader adoption of synthetic data as a practical foundation for layered design editing systems.

中文摘要翻译: 图像生成领域的最新进展使得生成高质量图像变得容易。然而，这些输出本质上是扁平化的，前景元素、背景和文本纠缠在固定画布中。因此，灵活的生成后编辑仍然具有挑战性，暴露出通向实际可用性的明显最后一公里差距。现有方法要么依赖稀缺的专有分层资产，要么从有限的结构先验构建部分合成数据。然而，这两种策略都面临基本的可扩展性挑战。在这项工作中，我们探究纯合成分层数据能否改进图形设计分解。我们假设，在图形设计中，有效的分解不需要像自然图像合成那样精确地建模层间依赖关系，因为设计元素通常被有意地安排为模块化和语义可分离的组件。具体而言，我们基于CLD基线（一种最先进的图层分解框架）开展了一项以数据为中心的研究。在该基线的基础上，我们构建了自己的合成数据集SynLayers，使用视觉语言模型生成文本监督信号，并利用VLM预测的边界框自动化推理输入。我们的研究揭示了三个关键发现：（1）即使是仅使用纯合成数据训练，也能超越非可扩展的替代方案（如广泛使用的PrismLayersPro数据集），证明了其作为可扩展且有效替代方案的可行性；（2）性能随训练数据规模的增加而一致提升，但增益在约5万个样本时开始趋于饱和；（3）合成数据能够实现图层数量分布的平衡控制，避免了真实数据集中常见的图层数量不平衡问题。我们希望这项以数据为中心的研究能够鼓励更广泛地将合成数据作为分层设计编辑系统的实用基础。

</details>


### [98] [Evidential Reasoning Advances Interpretable Real-World Disease Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15171)
*Chenyu Lian,Hong-Yu Zhou,Jing Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EviScreen是一个基于证据推理的疾病筛查框架，通过从双知识库中检索区域级历史病例证据，同时提升筛查性能与可解释性（回顾可解释性与定位可解释性），在临床级召回率下实现了更高的特异性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的医学图像疾病筛查模型存在两个主要问题：一是缺乏可解释性，无法提供透明的推理路径；二是性能欠佳，缺乏有效机制来参考历史病例证据。为此，作者提出EviScreen框架，旨在同时提升筛查性能和可解释性。

Method: EviScreen包含三个核心组件：1）双知识库（dual knowledge banks），用于存储和检索区域级历史病例证据；2）证据感知推理模块（evidence-aware reasoning module），结合当前病例与历史病例证据进行预测；3）对比检索生成的异常图（abnormality maps），用于增强定位可解释性，替代传统的后验显著性图。

Result: 在作者构建的真实世界疾病筛查基准上，EviScreen取得了优越的性能，尤其在临床级召回率下实现了显著更高的特异性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: EviScreen通过将历史病例的区域级证据融入推理过程，有效解决了现有筛查模型可解释性不足和性能欠佳的问题，为医学图像疾病筛查提供了一种同时具备回顾可解释性与定位可解释性的新范式。

Original Abstract: Disease screening is critical for early detection and timely intervention in clinical practice. However, most current screening models for medical images suffer from limited interpretability and suboptimal performance. They often lack effective mechanisms to reference historical cases or provide transparent reasoning pathways. To address these challenges, we introduce EviScreen, an evidential reasoning framework for disease screening that leverages region-level evidence from historical cases. The proposed EviScreen offers retrospection interpretability through regional evidence retrieved from dual knowledge banks. Using this evidential mechanism, the subsequent evidence-aware reasoning module makes predictions using both the current case and evidence from historical cases, thereby enhancing disease screening performance. Furthermore, rather than relying on post-hoc saliency maps, EviScreen enhances localization interpretability by leveraging abnormality maps derived from contrastive retrieval. Our method achieves superior performance on our carefully established benchmarks for real-world disease screening, yielding notably higher specificity at clinical-level recall. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/DopamineLcy/EviScreen.

中文摘要翻译: 疾病筛查对于临床实践中的早期检测和及时干预至关重要。然而，目前大多数基于医学图像的筛查模型存在可解释性有限和性能欠佳的问题，它们往往缺乏有效机制来参考历史病例或提供透明的推理路径。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了EviScreen——一种利用历史病例区域级证据进行证据推理的疾病筛查框架。所提出的EviScreen通过从双知识库中检索到的区域证据实现了回顾可解释性。基于这一证据机制，后续的证据感知推理模块同时利用当前病例和历史病例的证据进行预测，从而提升了疾病筛查性能。此外，EviScreen不依赖于后验显著性图，而是利用对比检索生成的异常图来增强定位可解释性。我们在精心构建的真实世界疾病筛查基准上取得了优越性能，在临床级召回率下实现了显著更高的特异性。代码已在https://github.com/DopamineLcy/EviScreen公开。

</details>


### [99] [SANA-WM: Efficient Minute-Scale World Modeling with Hybrid Linear Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15178)
*Haoyi Zhu,Haozhe Liu,Yuyang Zhao,Tian Ye,Junsong Chen,Jincheng Yu,Tong He,Song Han,Enze Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SANA-WM 是一个高效的开源世界模型（2.6B参数），原生训练用于生成一分钟长度、720p分辨率的高保真视频，并支持精确的6自由度相机控制。它通过混合线性注意力、双分支相机控制、两阶段生成流程和鲁棒标注管道四项核心设计，在仅使用约21.3万公开视频片段、64块H100训练15天的情况下，达到了与大规模工业基线相当的视觉质量，且吞吐量提升36倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有开源世界模型在长视频生成（分钟级）方面效率低下，且缺乏精确的相机控制能力；工业级基线虽然质量高但规模庞大、训练成本高。作者希望构建一个高效、开源、支持精确相机控制且能生成分钟级高质量视频的世界模型。

Method: (1) 混合线性注意力：将逐帧门控DeltaNet（GDN）与softmax注意力结合，实现内存高效的长上下文建模；(2) 双分支相机控制：确保精确的6自由度轨迹遵循；(3) 两阶段生成流程：对第一阶段输出应用长视频精炼器，提升序列质量与一致性；(4) 鲁棒标注管道：从公开视频中提取精确的度量级6-DoF相机位姿，生成高质量时空一致的动作标签。

Result: 仅使用约21.3万公开视频片段（含度量级位姿监督），在64块H100上训练15天；单个GPU即可生成60秒视频；蒸馏变体可在单张RTX 5090上通过NVFP4量化在34秒内去噪生成60秒720p视频；在一分钟世界模型基准上，动作跟随精度优于现有开源基线，视觉质量可比，吞吐量提升36倍。

Conclusion: SANA-WM证明了通过高效架构设计和数据标注策略，可以大幅降低世界模型的训练和部署成本，同时保持与大规模工业基线相当的视觉质量和更好的动作控制精度，为可扩展的世界建模提供了高效解决方案。

Original Abstract: We introduce SANA-WM, an efficient 2.6B-parameter open-source world model natively trained for one-minute generation, synthesizing high-fidelity, 720p, minute-scale videos with precise camera control. SANA-WM achieves visual quality comparable to large-scale industrial baselines such as LingBot-World and HY-WorldPlay, while significantly improving efficiency. Four core designs drive our architecture: (1) Hybrid Linear Attention combines frame-wise Gated DeltaNet (GDN) with softmax attention for memory-efficient long-context modeling. (2) Dual-Branch Camera Control ensures precise 6-DoF trajectory adherence. (3) Two-Stage Generation Pipeline applies a long-video refiner to stage-1 outputs, improving quality and consistency across sequences. (4) Robust Annotation Pipeline extracts accurate metric-scale 6-DoF camera poses from public videos to yield high-quality, spatiotemporally consistent action labels. Driven by these designs, SANA-WMdemonstrates remarkable efficiency across data, training compute, and inference hardware: it uses only $\sim$213K public video clips with metric-scale pose supervision, completes training in 15 days on 64 H100s, and generates each 60s clip on a single GPU; its distilled variant can be deployed on a single RTX 5090 with NVFP4 quantization to denoise a 60s 720p clip in 34s. On our one-minute world-model benchmark, SANA-WM demonstrates stronger action-following accuracy than prior open-source baselines and achieves comparable visual quality at $36\times$ higher throughput for scalable world modeling.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了SANA-WM，一个高效的26亿参数开源世界模型，原生训练用于生成一分钟时长、720p分辨率的高保真视频，并支持精确的相机控制。SANA-WM在视觉质量上达到了与LingBot-World和HY-WorldPlay等大规模工业基线相当的水平，同时显著提升了效率。四项核心设计驱动了我们的架构：(1) 混合线性注意力（Hybrid Linear Attention）将逐帧门控DeltaNet（GDN）与softmax注意力结合，实现了内存高效的长上下文建模。(2) 双分支相机控制（Dual-Branch Camera Control）确保了精确的6自由度轨迹跟随。(3) 两阶段生成流程（Two-Stage Generation Pipeline）对第一阶段输出应用长视频精炼器，提升了序列的质量和一致性。(4) 鲁棒标注管道（Robust Annotation Pipeline）从公开视频中提取精确的度量级6自由度相机位姿，生成了高质量、时空一致的动作标签。在这些设计的驱动下，SANA-WM在数据、训练计算和推理硬件方面展现了卓越的效率：它仅使用约21.3万带有度量级位姿监督的公开视频片段，在64块H100上完成训练仅需15天，且每个60秒片段可在单张GPU上生成；其蒸馏变体可通过NVFP4量化部署在单张RTX 5090上，在34秒内去噪生成60秒720p视频。在我们的一分钟世界模型基准上，SANA-WM展示了比现有开源基线更强的动作跟随精度，并在吞吐量提升36倍的情况下达到了可比的视觉质量，实现了可扩展的世界建模。

</details>


### [100] [From Plans to Pixels: Learning to Plan and Orchestrate for Open-Ended Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15181)
*Anirudh Sundara Rajan,Krishna Kumar Singh,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于经验学习的框架，通过规划器生成原子分解、协调器选择工具和区域执行编辑，并利用视觉语言评判器提供奖励来训练协调器、优化规划器，实现长时域图像编辑。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像编辑模型能产生逼真结果，但难以处理抽象、多步骤指令；而基于智能体的方法依赖手工流程或教师模仿，缺乏灵活性，且学习与编辑结果脱节。

Method: 提出经验性框架：规划器生成结构化原子分解；协调器为每一步选择工具和编辑区域；视觉语言评判器基于结果提供指令遵循度和视觉质量的奖励；协调器通过最大化奖励进行训练，成功轨迹用于优化规划器。

Result: 该方法相比单步编辑或基于规则的多步基线方法，能够产生更连贯、更可靠的编辑结果。

Conclusion: 将规划与奖励驱动的执行紧密耦合，能有效实现长时域、抽象多步图像编辑任务。

Original Abstract: Modern image editing models produce realistic results but struggle with abstract, multi step instructions (e.g., ``make this advertisement more vegetarian-friendly''). Prior agent based methods decompose such tasks but rely on handcrafted pipelines or teacher imitation, limiting flexibility and decoupling learning from actual editing outcomes. We propose an experiential framework for long-horizon image editing, where a planner generates structured atomic decompositions and an orchestrator selects tools and regions to execute each step. A vision language judge provides outcome-based rewards for instruction adherence and visual quality. The orchestrator is trained to maximize these rewards, and successful trajectories are used to refine the planner. By tightly coupling planning with reward driven execution, our approach yields more coherent and reliable edits than single-step or rule-based multistep baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 现代图像编辑模型能够产生逼真的结果，但在处理抽象的、多步骤的指令（例如，“让这个广告看起来更符合素食主义风格”）时存在困难。以往基于智能体的方法虽然可以分解此类任务，但依赖于手工设计的流程或教师模仿，这限制了灵活性，并且使得学习与实际编辑结果脱节。我们提出了一种用于长时域图像编辑的经验性框架，其中规划器生成结构化的原子分解，协调器选择工具和区域来执行每一步。一个视觉语言评判器基于编辑结果提供关于指令遵循度和视觉质量的奖励。协调器通过最大化这些奖励进行训练，成功轨迹则被用于优化规划器。通过将规划与奖励驱动的执行紧密耦合，我们的方法相比单步编辑或基于规则的多步基线方法，能够产生更连贯、更可靠的编辑结果。

</details>


### [101] [Warp-as-History: Generalizable Camera-Controlled Video Generation from One Training Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15182)
*Yifan Wang,Tong He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Warp-as-History接口，通过将相机运动产生的扭曲帧作为伪历史输入冻结的视频生成模型，无需训练或测试时优化即可实现零样本的相机可控视频生成，轻量级LoRA微调可进一步提升效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有相机可控视频生成方法通常需要针对相机特定条件进行后训练（如相机编码器、控制分支等），或依赖测试时优化。本文旨在无需额外训练、架构修改或测试时优化的条件下，让冻结的视频生成模型直接具备遵循相机轨迹的零样本能力。

Method: 提出Warp-as-History接口：根据目标相机轨迹，将历史观测帧通过相机扭曲（warp）构造为相机扭曲的伪历史帧，实现目标帧的位置对齐和可见令牌选择，然后将该伪历史帧通过模型原有的视觉历史通路输入，同时对齐其位置编码与正在去噪的目标帧，并移除无效源观测的扭曲历史令牌。

Result: 在多种数据集上的大量实验验证了该方法无需任何训练即可实现零样本相机轨迹遵循；轻量级离线LoRA微调（仅需一个带相机标注的视频）可进一步提升相机遵循度、视觉质量和运动动态，并泛化到未见视频。

Conclusion: Warp-as-History作为简单接口，在不需训练、架构修改或测试时优化的前提下，赋予了冻结视频生成模型零样本的相机可控能力，且通过极轻量的微调可进一步提升性能，为相机可控视频生成提供了一种高效实用的新范式。

Original Abstract: Camera-controlled video generation has made substantial progress, enabling generated videos to follow prescribed viewpoint trajectories. However, existing methods usually learn camera-specific conditioning through camera encoders, control branches, or attention and positional-encoding modifications, which often require post-training on large-scale camera-annotated videos. Training-free alternatives avoid such post-training, but often shift the cost to test-time optimization or extra denoising-time guidance. We propose Warp-as-History, a simple interface that turns camera-induced warps into camera-warped pseudo-history with target-frame positional alignment and visible-token selection. Given a target camera trajectory, we construct camera-warped pseudo-history from past observations and feed it through the model's visual-history pathway. Crucially, we align its positional encoding with the target frames being denoised and remove warped-history tokens without valid source observations. Without any training, architectural modification, or test-time optimization, this interface reveals a non-trivial zero-shot capability of a frozen video generation model to follow camera trajectories. Moreover, lightweight offline LoRA finetuning on only one camera-annotated video further improves this capability and generalizes to unseen videos, improving camera adherence, visual quality, and motion dynamics without test-time optimization or target-video adaptation. Extensive experiments on diverse datasets confirm the effectiveness of our method.

中文摘要翻译: 相机可控的视频生成已取得实质性进展，使生成的视频能够遵循指定的视角轨迹。然而，现有方法通常通过相机编码器、控制分支或注意力和位置编码修改来学习相机特定的条件控制，这通常需要在大型相机标注视频上进行后训练。无需训练的方法避免了这种后训练，但往往将成本转移到测试时优化或额外的去噪时引导上。本文提出Warp-as-History（扭曲即历史），一个简单的接口，将相机诱导的扭曲转换为相机扭曲的伪历史帧，并实现目标帧的位置对齐和可见令牌选择。给定目标相机轨迹，我们从历史观测中构建相机扭曲的伪历史帧，并通过模型的视觉历史通路输入。关键在于，我们将其位置编码与正在去噪的目标帧对齐，并移除没有有效源观测的扭曲历史令牌。无需任何训练、架构修改或测试时优化，该接口揭示了冻结视频生成模型遵循相机轨迹的非平凡零样本能力。此外，仅在一个相机标注视频上进行轻量级离线LoRA微调，即可进一步提升该能力并泛化到未见视频，在无需测试时优化或目标视频适配的情况下，改进了相机遵循度、视觉质量和运动动态。在多种数据集上的大量实验证实了该方法的有效性。

</details>


### [102] [Quantitative Video World Model Evaluation for Geometric-Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15185)
*Jiaxin Wu,Yihao Pi,Yinling Zhang,Yuheng Li,Xueyan Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PDI-Bench定量评估框架，通过分割、点跟踪和3D重建计算投影几何残差，检测生成视频中尺度-深度对齐、3D运动一致性和3D结构刚性三个维度的几何失效。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频评估依赖人工判断或学习型评分器，主观性强且对几何失效诊断能力弱，缺乏定量评估生成视频物理合理性的方法。

Method: 对生成视频进行分割和点跟踪（SAM 2、MegaSaM、CoTracker3），通过单目重建提升到3D世界坐标，计算包含尺度-深度对齐、3D运动一致性和3D结构刚性三个维度的投影几何残差。

Result: 在PDI-Dataset上评估多种SOTA视频生成器，PDI揭示出常见感知指标无法捕获的几何专用失效模式。

Conclusion: PDI为物理合理视频生成和世界模型进展提供了诊断信号。

Original Abstract: Generative video models are increasingly studied as implicit world models, yet evaluating whether they produce physically plausible 3D structure and motion remains challenging. Most existing video evaluation pipelines rely heavily on human judgment or learned graders, which can be subjective and weakly diagnostic for geometric failures. We introduce PDI-Bench (Perspective Distortion Index), a quantitative framework for auditing geometric coherence in generated videos. Given a generated clip, we obtain object-centric observations via segmentation and point tracking (e.g., SAM 2, MegaSaM, and CoTracker3), lift them to 3D world-space coordinates via monocular reconstruction, and compute a set of projective-geometry residuals capturing three failure dimensions: scale-depth alignment, 3D motion consistency, and 3D structural rigidity. To support systematic evaluation, we build PDI-Dataset, covering diverse scenarios designed to stress these geometric constraints. Across state-of-the-art video generators, PDI reveals consistent geometry-specific failure modes that are not captured by common perceptual metrics, and provides a diagnostic signal for progress toward physically grounded video generation and physical world model. Our code and dataset can be found at https://pdi-bench.github.io/.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式视频模型正日益被研究作为隐式世界模型，然而评估其是否生成物理合理的3D结构和运动仍然具有挑战性。现有大多数视频评估流程严重依赖人工判断或学习型评分器，这些方法具有主观性且对几何失效的诊断能力弱。我们提出PDI-Bench（透视畸变指数），一个用于审计生成视频几何一致性的定量框架。给定一个生成片段，我们通过分割和点跟踪（例如SAM 2、MegaSaM和CoTracker3）获取以对象为中心的观测，通过单目重建将其提升到3D世界空间坐标，并计算一组投影几何残差，涵盖三个失效维度：尺度-深度对齐、3D运动一致性和3D结构刚性。为支持系统性评估，我们构建了PDI-Dataset，覆盖旨在挑战这些几何约束的多样化场景。在多个最先进的视频生成器上，PDI揭示了常见的感知指标无法捕获的、几何特有的失效模式，并为迈向物理合理的视频生成及物理世界模型的进展提供了诊断信号。我们的代码和数据集可在https://pdi-bench.github.io/获取。

</details>


### [103] [Articraft: An Agentic System for Scalable Articulated 3D Asset Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15187)
*Matt Zhou,Ruining Li,Xiaoyang Lyu,Zhaomou Song,Zhening Huang,Chuanxia Zheng,Christian Rupprecht,Andrea Vedaldi,Shangzhe Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Articraft系统，利用大语言模型自动编写程序生成铰接式3D资产，并创建包含超过1万个资产的数据集Articraft-10K，在质量和规模上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 学习理解铰接式3D物体的瓶颈在于缺乏大规模且多样化的数据集，现有数据集规模有限，亟需自动化生成高质量铰接资产的方法。

Method: 将铰接式3D资产生成问题转化为编写程序构建该资产的问题，提出Articraft智能体系统：LLM基于领域特定SDK编写代码定义部件、组合几何体、指定关节并编写测试脚本，系统提供受控工作空间和结构化反馈，自动验证生成资产并返回错误信息供LLM迭代改进。

Result: 1) Articraft生成的资产质量优于最先进的铰接资产生成器和通用编码智能体；2) 构建了Articraft-10K数据集，包含超过1万个铰接资产，覆盖245个类别；3) 该数据集可用于训练铰接资产模型以及机器人仿真、虚拟现实等下游应用。

Conclusion: 利用LLM通过程序化接口自动生成铰接3D资产是可行且高效的，Articraft在质量和规模上均超越现有方法，生成的大规模数据集对相关研究和应用具有重要价值。

Original Abstract: A bottleneck in learning to understand articulated 3D objects is the lack of large and diverse datasets. In this paper, we propose to leverage large language models (LLMs) to close this gap and generate articulated assets at scale. We reduce the problem of generating an articulated 3D asset to that of writing a program that builds it. We then introduce a new agentic system, Articraft, that writes such programs automatically. We design a programmatic interface and harness to help the LLM do so effectively. The LLM writes code against a domain-specific SDK for defining parts, composing geometry, specifying joints, and writing tests to validate the resulting assets. The harness exposes a restricted workspace and interface to the LLM, validates the resulting assets, and returns structured feedback. In this way, the LLM is not distracted by details such as authoring a URDF file or managing a complex software environment. We show that this produces higher-quality assets than both state-of-the-art articulated-asset generators and general-purpose coding agents. Using Articraft, we build Articraft-10K, a curated dataset of over 10K articulated assets spanning 245 categories, and show its utility both for training models of articulated assets and in downstream applications such as robotics simulation and virtual reality.

中文摘要翻译: 学习理解铰接式3D物体的一个瓶颈在于缺乏大规模且多样化的数据集。本文提出利用大语言模型来弥合这一差距，从而实现铰接资产的大规模生成。我们将生成铰接式3D资产的问题简化为编写构建该资产的程序。为此，我们引入了一个新的智能体系统——Articraft，它可以自动编写此类程序。我们设计了一套程序化接口和辅助框架，以帮助大语言模型有效地完成这一任务。大语言模型针对一个领域特定SDK编写代码，用于定义部件、组合几何体、指定关节，并编写测试脚本来验证生成的资产。辅助框架为LLM提供了受限的工作空间和接口，验证生成的资产，并返回结构化反馈。通过这种方式，大语言模型不会被诸如编写URDF文件或管理复杂软件环境等细节所干扰。实验表明，该方法生成的资产质量优于最先进的铰接资产生成器和通用编码智能体。利用Articraft，我们构建了Articraft-10K，一个经过筛选的、涵盖245个类别、超过1万个铰接资产的数据集，并展示了其在训练铰接资产模型以及机器人仿真和虚拟现实等下游应用中的实用价值。

</details>


### [104] [Aligning Latent Geometry for Spherical Flow Matching in Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15193)
*Tuna Han Salih Meral,Kaan Oktay,Hidir Yesiltepe,Adil Kaan Akan,Pinar Yanardag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过径向归一化和球面线性插值替代传统欧几里得线性路径，使得潜在流匹配的轨迹始终保持在球面上，从而提升图像生成质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统潜在流匹配中，高斯噪声和VAE潜在变量都集中在薄球壳上，欧几里得弦会偏离这些球壳，导致生成路径不理想。本文希望通过修正路径来改善生成效果。

Method: 1) 将每个潜在token分解为径向和角度分量，通过分量交换探针实验发现语义内容主要由方向承载；2) 将数据潜在投影到固定token半径上，将高斯噪声的径向投影作为球面先验；3) 冻结编码器微调解码器；4) 用球面线性插值（SLERP）替换线性插值，使路径始终保持在球面上，速度目标纯角度化。

Result: 在ImageNet-256类别条件生成任务上，该方法在不同图像分词器下一致改善了FID指标，且无需修改扩散架构，也不需要辅助编码器或表示对齐目标。

Conclusion: 通过将流匹配路径约束在球面上的简单几何修正，即可显著提升图像生成性能，验证了方向信息在潜在表示中起主导作用的发现。

Original Abstract: Latent flow matching for image generation usually transports Gaussian noise to variational autoencoder latents along linear paths. Both endpoints, however, concentrate in thin spherical shells, and a Euclidean chord leaves those shells even when preprocessing aligns their radii. By decomposing each latent token into radial and angular components, we show through component-swap probes that decoded perceptual and semantic content is carried predominantly by direction, with radius contributing much less. We therefore project data latents onto a fixed token radius, use the radial projection of Gaussian noise as the spherical prior, finetune the decoder with the encoder frozen, and replace linear interpolation with spherical linear interpolation. The resulting geodesic paths stay on the sphere at every timestep, and their velocity targets are purely angular by construction. Under matched training, the method consistently improves class-conditional ImageNet-256 FID across different image tokenizers, leaves the diffusion architecture unchanged, and requires no auxiliary encoder or representation-alignment objective.

中文摘要翻译: 用于图像生成的潜在流匹配通常将高斯噪声沿线性路径传输到变分自编码器的潜在表示。然而，两个端点的分布都集中在薄球壳上，即使通过预处理对齐了半径，欧几里得弦仍然会偏离这些球壳。通过将每个潜在token分解为径向和角度分量，我们利用分量交换探针实验发现，解码后的感知和语义内容主要由方向承载，半径贡献很小。因此，我们将数据潜在投影到固定token半径上，将高斯噪声的径向投影作为球面先验，在冻结编码器的同时微调解码器，并用球面线性插值替换线性插值。由此产生的测地线路径在每个时间步都保持在球面上，其速度目标由构造保证是纯角度的。在匹配训练条件下，该方法在不同图像分词器上一致改善了类别条件ImageNet-256的FID指标，不改变扩散架构，也不需要辅助编码器或表示对齐目标。

</details>


### [105] [ATLAS: Agentic or Latent Visual Reasoning? One Word is Enough for Both](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15198)
*Ziyu Guo,Rain Liu,Xinyan Chen,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ATLAS框架，将单个离散"功能标记"同时作为智能体操作和潜在视觉推理单元，结合两者优势，避免冗长的中间视觉内容生成，兼容标准SFT和RL训练，并引入LA-GRPO稳定强化学习训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉推理方法存在不足：直接生成图像计算成本高且架构复杂；智能体方法通过代码或工具调用导致外部执行延迟；潜在推理方法缺乏任务泛化能力且难以用自回归并行化训练。为结合智能体方法和潜在推理方法的优势并克服其局限，提出ATLAS。

Method: 提出ATLAS框架，核心是"功能标记"（functional token）——一个离散的单个"词"同时作为智能体操作和潜在视觉推理单元。每个功能标记关联内部化的视觉操作，无需视觉监督，是分词器词汇表中的标准标记，通过下一标记预测生成。设计避免冗长的中间视觉内容生成，兼容标准SFT和RL训练。进一步提出潜在锚定GRPO（LA-GRPO），通过静态加权辅助目标锚定功能标记，提供更强梯度更新以稳定强化学习训练。

Result: 大量实验和分析表明，ATLAS在具有挑战性的基准测试中取得了优越性能，同时保持了清晰的可解释性。

Conclusion: ATLAS为未来视觉推理研究提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: Visual reasoning, often interleaved with intermediate visual states, has emerged as a promising direction in the field. A straightforward approach is to directly generate images via unified models during reasoning, but this is computationally expensive and architecturally non-trivial. Recent alternatives include agentic reasoning through code or tool calls, and latent reasoning with learnable hidden embeddings. However, agentic methods incur context-switching latency from external execution, while latent methods lack task generalization and are difficult to train with autoregressive parallelization. To combine their strengths while mitigating their limitations, we propose ATLAS, a framework in which a single discrete 'word', termed as a functional token, serves both as an agentic operation and a latent visual reasoning unit. Each functional token is associated with an internalized visual operation, yet requires no visual supervision and remains a standard token in the tokenizer vocabulary, which can be generated via next-token prediction. This design avoids verbose intermediate visual content generation, while preserving compatibility with the vanilla scalable SFT and RL training, without architectural or methodological modifications. To further address the sparsity of functional tokens during RL, we introduce Latent-Anchored GRPO (LA-GRPO), which stabilizes the training by anchoring functional tokens with a statically weighted auxiliary objective, providing stronger gradient updates. Extensive experiments and analyses demonstrate that ATLAS achieves superior performance on challenging benchmarks while maintaining clear interpretability. We hope ATLAS offers a new paradigm inspiring future visual reasoning research.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉推理通常与中间视觉状态交错进行，已成为该领域一个有前景的方向。一种直接的方法是在推理过程中通过统一模型直接生成图像，但这在计算上昂贵且架构上不简单。最近的替代方案包括通过代码或工具调用的智能体推理，以及使用可学习隐藏嵌入的潜在推理。然而，智能体方法因外部执行而产生上下文切换延迟，而潜在方法缺乏任务泛化能力，且难以用自回归并行化进行训练。为了结合两者的优势同时缓解其局限性，我们提出了ATLAS，这是一个框架，其中单个离散的"词"（称为功能标记）同时充当智能体操作和潜在视觉推理单元。每个功能标记关联一个内部化的视觉操作，但无需视觉监督，并且是分词器词汇表中的一个标准标记，可以通过下一标记预测生成。这种设计避免了冗长的中间视觉内容生成，同时保持与标准SFT和RL训练的兼容性，无需架构或方法上的修改。为了进一步解决强化学习过程中功能标记的稀疏性问题，我们引入了潜在锚定GRPO（LA-GRPO），通过一个静态加权的辅助目标锚定功能标记来稳定训练，提供更强的梯度更新。大量实验和分析表明，ATLAS在具有挑战性的基准测试上取得了优越的性能，同时保持了清晰的可解释性。我们希望ATLAS能为未来的视觉推理研究提供一种新范式。

</details>


### [106] [EntityBench: Towards Entity-Consistent Long-Range Multi-Shot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15199)
*Ruozhen He,Meng Wei,Ziyan Yang,Vicente Ordonez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EntityBench基准（140个剧情、2491个镜头）和EntityMem记忆增强系统，系统性地评估和改善多镜头视频生成中的跨镜头实体一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频生成评估使用独立生成的提示集，实体覆盖有限且一致性指标简单，难以标准化比较长序列中角色、物体和位置的一致性。

Method: 构建EntityBench基准（来自真实叙事媒体的140个剧情/2491个镜头，含显式逐镜头实体调度表，分易/中/难三档），配套三支柱评估体系（帧内质量、提示对齐、跨镜头一致性）及保真门控机制；提出EntityMem记忆增强系统，在生成前将已验证的逐实体视觉参考存储到持久记忆库中。

Result: 跨镜头实体一致性随重现距离增加而急剧下降；显式逐实体记忆方法在角色保真度（Cohen's d=+2.33）和出现率上达到最高。

Conclusion: 现有方法在长序列跨镜头一致性上存在显著退化，而EntityMem通过显式逐实体记忆机制有效提升了角色一致性。

Original Abstract: Multi-shot video generation extends single-shot generation to coherent visual narratives, yet maintaining consistent characters, objects, and locations across shots remains a challenge over long sequences. Existing evaluations typically use independently generated prompt sets with limited entity coverage and simple consistency metrics, making standardized comparison difficult. We introduce EntityBench, a benchmark of 140 episodes (2,491 shots) derived from real narrative media, with explicit per-shot entity schedules tracking characters, objects, and locations simultaneously across easy / medium / hard tiers of up to 50 shots, 13 cross-shot characters, 8 cross-shot locations, 22 cross-shot objects, and recurrence gaps spanning up to 48 shots. It is paired with a three-pillar evaluation suite that disentangles intra-shot quality, prompt-following alignment, and cross-shot consistency, with a fidelity gate that admits only accurate entity appearances into cross-shot scoring. As a baseline, we propose EntityMem, a memory-augmented generation system that stores verified per-entity visual references in a persistent memory bank before generation begins. Experiments show that cross-shot entity consistency degrades sharply with recurrence distance in existing methods, and that explicit per-entity memory yields the highest character fidelity (Cohen's d = +2.33) and presence among methods evaluated. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Catherine-R-He/EntityBench/.

中文摘要翻译: 多镜头视频生成将单镜头生成扩展为连贯的视觉叙事，但在长序列中保持角色、物体和位置的一致性仍是一个挑战。现有评估通常使用独立生成的提示集，实体覆盖有限且一致性指标简单，难以进行标准化比较。本文引入EntityBench，一个基于真实叙事媒体构建的基准，包含140个剧情（2491个镜头），具有显式的逐镜头实体调度表，可同时在易/中/难三个难度层级中追踪角色、物体和位置，最多涉及50个镜头、13个跨镜头角色、8个跨镜头位置、22个跨镜头物体，重现间隔跨度可达48个镜头。该基准配有一个三支柱评估套件，将帧内质量、提示跟随对齐和跨镜头一致性解耦，并设有保真门控机制，仅允许准确的实体外观进入跨镜头评分。作为基线方法，我们提出EntityMem，一个记忆增强生成系统，在生成之前将验证过的逐实体视觉参考存储到持久记忆库中。实验表明，现有方法的跨镜头实体一致性随重现距离增加而急剧下降，而显式的逐实体记忆在所有被评估方法中实现了最高的角色保真度（Cohen's d = +2.33）和出现率。代码和数据已发布于https://github.com/Catherine-R-He/EntityBench/。

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [Weight Hybrid Architecture of Rydberg-Atomic Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14474)
*Hao Wu,Xinyuan Yao,Shanchi Wu,Rui Ni,Chen Gong,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种权重混合（WH）架构，通过四通道联合处理（双信号通道+双噪声参考通道）来抑制里德伯原子量子接收机中激光等硬件引入的相关噪声，并采用期望最大化框架下的最大似然估计实现最优通道组合。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 里德伯原子量子接收机在无线电通信接收中具有高灵敏度优势，但硬件噪声（尤其是激光噪声）会显著降低其性能，且这类噪声具有相关性，传统方法难以有效抑制。

Method: 提出权重混合（WH）架构，一种四通道联合方案：双信号通道与双噪声参考通道协同处理，在期望最大化（EM）框架下通过最大似然估计（MLE）对多通道进行最优加权合并，从而在相关噪声环境中实现鲁棒的信号提取。

Result: 所提出的WH架构能有效抑制激光及其他硬件组件的噪声贡献，实现相关噪声环境下的鲁棒信号提取，且该架构具有通用性，可扩展至其他类型的里德伯接收机。

Conclusion: 本文提出的权重混合（WH）架构为里德伯原子传感器在相关噪声环境下的信号接收提供了一种有效方案，具有通用性和可扩展性，可用于各类里德伯接收机以持续提升性能。

Original Abstract: Rydberg atomic quantum receivers have been seen as novel radio frequency measurements and the high sensitivity to a large range of frequencies makes it attractive for communications reception. However, their performance can be significantly degraded by hardware-induced noise, particularly the noise from laser, which impacts the overall system noise floor and exhibits correlation. To address this challenge, this paper proposes a weight hybrid (WH) architecture for Rydberg-atomic sensors, a novel four-channel combining scheme designed for atomic sensors operating in correlated noise environments. By jointly processing dual signal channels and dual noise reference channels, the WH architecture effectively mitigates noise contributions from lasers and other hardware components. All channels are optimally combined via maximum likelihood estimation within an expectation maximization framework, enabling robust signal extraction under correlated noise. Moreover, the proposed WH architecture is universal and can be readily extended to other types of Rydberg receivers to achieve consistent performance improvements.

中文摘要翻译: 里德伯原子量子接收机被视为一种新型射频测量技术，其对宽频段信号的高灵敏度使其在通信接收领域具有吸引力。然而，硬件引入的噪声（尤其是激光噪声）会显著降低其性能，这类噪声会影响整体系统噪声基底并呈现相关性。为应对这一挑战，本文提出了一种面向里德伯原子传感器的权重混合（WH）架构——一种专为在相关噪声环境下工作的原子传感器设计的四通道合并方案。通过联合处理双信号通道和双噪声参考通道，WH架构有效抑制了激光及其他硬件组件引入的噪声贡献。所有通道在期望最大化框架下通过最大似然估计进行最优合并，从而在相关噪声下实现鲁棒的信号提取。此外，所提出的WH架构具有通用性，可便捷地扩展到其他类型的里德伯接收机，以实现持续的性能提升。

</details>


### [108] [WHTDM: Walsh-Hadamard Transform Division Multiplexing for Doubly-Selective Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14482)
*Wang Hao,Yuan Zhonghao,Chi Yonggang,Zhang Kuang,Tan Chenxing,Yu Jiaxing*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出Walsh-Hadamard变换分复用（WHTDM），用实值Walsh-Hadamard变换替代OFDM中的IFFT/FFT，结合跨域记忆近似消息传递（CD-MAMP）均衡器，在高速移动场景下显著优于传统OFDM，且发射机复杂度更低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 降低OFDM发射机的计算复杂度，消除变换阶段的复数乘法，使其更适合低功耗物联网终端，同时保持或提升在高移动性双选信道下的检测性能。

Method: 提出Walsh-Hadamard变换分复用（WHTDM）多载波波形，用实值酉Walsh-Hadamard变换替代IFFT/FFT，消除变换阶段所有复数乘法；采用跨域记忆近似消息传递（CD-MAMP）均衡器，利用等效WHT域信道矩阵的带状结构进行检测。

Result: 在28 GHz 3GPP TDL-C信道模型下，WHTDM结合CD-MAMP在120 km/h高速移动下BER比传统OFDM 1-tap MMSE低一个数量级以上；在CD-MAMP均衡的新波形中，WHTDM的BER性能最佳，发射机复杂度比OFDM低2.5倍，且完全消除变换阶段的复数乘法器。

Conclusion: WHTDM在保持优异BER性能的同时大幅降低发射机复杂度，非常适合低功耗物联网终端应用。

Original Abstract: We propose Walsh-Hadamard Transform Division Multiplexing (WHTDM), a multicarrier waveform that replaces the conventional IFFT/FFT pair in OFDM with a real-valued, unitary Walsh-Hadamard transform (WHT). WHTDM inherits the CP-OFDM transceiver structure while eliminating all complex multiplications from the transform stage, yielding a transmitter with zero real multipliers in the core modulation block. For detection under doubly-selective channels, we adopt a cross-domain memory approximate message passing (CD-MAMP) equalizer that operates on the banded structure of the equivalent WHT-domain channel matrix. Simulation results under the 3GPP TDL-C channel model at 28 GHz demonstrate that WHTDM with CD-MAMP significantly outperforms conventional OFDM 1-tap MMSE at high mobility, achieving over an order of magnitude lower BER at 120 km/h. Among the compared CD-MAMP-equalized new waveforms, WHTDM achieves the best BER performance while maintaining a transmitter complexity 2.5 $\times$ lower than OFDM and completely eliminating complex multipliers from the transform stage, making it well-suited for low-power IoT terminals.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了Walsh-Hadamard变换分复用（WHTDM），一种多载波波形，它用实值、酉的Walsh-Hadamard变换（WHT）替代OFDM中传统的IFFT/FFT对。WHTDM继承了CP-OFDM的收发机结构，同时消除了变换阶段的所有复数乘法，使得核心调制模块中发射机具有零实数乘法器。针对双选信道下的检测问题，我们采用了一种跨域记忆近似消息传递（CD-MAMP）均衡器，该均衡器利用等效WHT域信道矩阵的带状结构进行工作。在28 GHz的3GPP TDL-C信道模型下的仿真结果表明，配备CD-MAMP的WHTDM在高移动性场景下显著优于传统OFDM 1-tap MMSE均衡器，在120 km/h时实现了超过一个数量级的误码率降低。在采用CD-MAMP均衡的新波形中，WHTDM在获得最佳误码率性能的同时，发射机复杂度比OFDM低2.5倍，并完全消除了变换阶段的复数乘法器，使其非常适合低功耗物联网终端。

</details>


### [109] [Multimodal Learning for MIMO Beam Prediction Based on Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14650)
*Zijian Zheng,Wenqiang Yi,Hyundong Shin,Arumugam Nallanathan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于变分推断的多模态框架，通过将优化问题解耦为模块化特征提取和跨模态语义对齐，并采用两阶段训练策略（先利用单模态数据学习表征，再在有限多模态样本上精调对齐），在仅使用传统端到端方法20%多模态训练数据的情况下，实现了具有竞争力的波束预测精度和高可靠性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在集成感知与通信的Massive MIMO系统中，准确波束预测对降低信令开销和延迟至关重要。多模态学习虽能利用其他传感器的互补信息提升预测精度，但实际部署中获取语义对齐的多模态数据集成本高昂，限制了其实用性。

Method: 提出基于变分推断的多模态框架，将优化问题解耦为模块化特征提取和跨模态语义对齐两个子问题；开发两阶段训练策略：第一阶段利用丰富的单模态数据进行表示学习，第二阶段在有限的多模态样本上进行精细化语义对齐，从而提升数据效率并确保在感知不确定性下的鲁棒特征融合。

Result: 在DeepSense6G数据集上的实验结果表明，所提框架在仅需传统端到端基准方法20%多模态训练数据的情况下，实现了具有竞争力的波束预测精度并保持高可靠性。

Conclusion: 所提出的变分推断多模态框架通过解耦优化和两阶段训练策略，有效降低了多模态数据采集成本，在显著减少多模态训练数据需求的同时，仍能保持良好的波束预测性能与可靠性，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Accurate beam prediction is essential for mitigating signalling overhead and latency in integrated sensing and communication-enabled massive multi-input multi-output systems. With the aid of multimodal learning, the prediction accuracy can be enhanced by leveraging the complementary information from other existing sensors, but the practical deployment is often constrained by the high cost of acquiring semantically aligned multimodal datasets. This paper proposes a variational-inference-based multimodal framework that decouples the optimization problem into modular feature extraction and cross-modal semantic alignment. Specifically, we develop a two-stage training strategy where the model utilises abundant unimodal data for representation learning before performing refined alignment on limited multimodal samples. This design enhances data efficiency and ensures robust feature fusion under sensing uncertainties. Experimental results on the DeepSense6G dataset demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves competitive beam prediction accuracy and maintains high reliability, while only requiring 20% of the multimodal training data compared to conventional end-to-end benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 准确的波束预测对于降低集成感知与通信的Massive MIMO系统中的信令开销和延迟至关重要。借助多模态学习，可以利用来自其他现有传感器的互补信息来提升预测精度，但实际部署往往受限于获取语义对齐的多模态数据集的高昂成本。本文提出了一种基于变分推断的多模态框架，将优化问题解耦为模块化特征提取和跨模态语义对齐。具体而言，我们开发了一种两阶段训练策略：模型首先利用丰富的单模态数据进行表示学习，随后在有限的多模态样本上执行精细化对齐。这种设计提升了数据效率，并确保了在感知不确定性下的鲁棒特征融合。在DeepSense6G数据集上的实验结果表明，所提出的框架在仅需传统端到端基准方法20%多模态训练数据的情况下，达到了具有竞争力的波束预测精度并保持了高可靠性。

</details>


### [110] [Joint Phase Noise and Channel Estimation for OTFS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14720)
*Stephen McWade,Arman Farhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了OTFS系统中振荡器相位噪声的影响，分析了时延-多普勒域中的相位噪声干扰，推导了三种振荡器类型的SINR表达式，并提出了一种基于维纳滤波的联合信道与相位噪声估计方法，相比现有方法可获得高达8 dB的BER增益。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: OTFS系统对相位噪声敏感，现有研究仅考虑CPE的相位噪声估计方法无法有效补偿严重的多普勒间干扰（IDI），且现有方法假设信道为已知单抽头信道，缺乏实用性。

Method: 提出了一种基于维纳滤波的联合信道与相位噪声估计方法，利用相位噪声和多普勒扩展信道的统计特性进行联合估计。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提方法在误码率（BER）性能上比现有方法提升高达8 dB。

Conclusion: OTFS对相位噪声敏感，需适当的估计与补偿；所提维纳滤波方法能有效联合估计信道和相位噪声，显著提升系统性能。

Original Abstract: This paper investigates the effect of oscillator phase noise in orthogonal time frequency space (OTFS) systems. The paper provides in-depth analysis of the interference due to phase noise in the delay-Doppler domain and derives expressions for SINR for three different oscillator types, namely free-running oscillators, continuous-time phase locked loops (PLLs) and discrete-time PLLs. The analysis demonstrates the OTFS is sensitive to phase noise and requires appropriate estimation and compensation. In particular, the analysis shows phase noise imposed inter-Doppler-interference (IDI) is severe and that existing phase noise estimation techniques which only consider the common-phase-error (CPE) can not compensate this IDI effectively. Additionally, the existing methods in the OTFS literature on phase noise assume the channel to be a known single tap channel. Hence, in this paper, we propose a method for joint channel and phase noise estimation using a Wiener filtering approach. Our proposed method exploits the statistical nature of both the phase noise and the Doppler spread channel. Our numerical results demonstrate the superior performance of our proposed technique, with gains of up to 8~dB in terms of bit error rate (BER) over existing methods in the literature.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了正交时频空间（OTFS）系统中振荡器相位噪声的影响。论文深入分析了时延-多普勒域中由相位噪声引起的干扰，并推导了三种振荡器类型（即自由运行振荡器、连续时间锁相环和离散时间锁相环）的SINR表达式。分析表明OTFS对相位噪声敏感，需要进行适当的估计和补偿。特别地，分析显示相位噪声引起的多普勒间干扰（IDI）是严重的，且仅考虑公共相位误差（CPE）的现有相位噪声估计技术无法有效补偿这种IDI。此外，OTFS文献中现有的相位噪声方法假设信道是已知的单抽头信道。因此，本文提出了一种基于维纳滤波的联合信道与相位噪声估计方法。我们提出的方法利用了相位噪声和多普勒扩展信道的统计特性。数值结果表明，所提技术具有优越的性能，在误码率（BER）方面相比现有方法可获得高达8 dB的增益。

</details>


### [111] [Making AFDM Secure Against Eavesdroppers: A Phase Function Design Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14837)
*Hengxuan Liu,Vincent Savaux,Arman Farhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文通过设计一种通用相位函数作为AFDM的第二个啁啾参数，显著增强了波形对窃听者暴力解调的抵抗能力，将物理层安全性能提升了数个数量级。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统AFDM波形虽然利用两个可调参数分别应对多普勒效应和物理层安全，但第二个参数的现有设计在对抗窃听者暴力解调方面的安全性仍有不足，亟需提升其鲁棒性。

Method: 首先推导了暴力解调复杂度取决于相位函数一阶导数的设计准则；然后引入了一类能在保持AFDM啁啾结构的前提下，以无界且可控的方式提升暴力解调复杂度的相位函数族。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的相位函数设计在暴力解调复杂度方面将AFDM的物理层安全性能提升了数个数量级，相比传统AFDM具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的通用相位函数设计能够有效增强AFDM波形对抗窃听者暴力解调的能力，为高机动通信场景下的物理层安全提供了一种可行且高效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM) has recently emerged as a promising waveform for high-mobility communications due to its resilience to Doppler effects and its advantages for integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). AFDM modulates transmit data symbols using chirp subcarriers with two adjustable parameters. One is used for dealing with the Doppler effect and the second parameter can be used for physical layer security (PLS). In this paper, we focus on designing the second chirp parameter in the form of a generic phase function to enhance the robustness of the waveform against brute-force demodulation by the eavesdropper. In particular, we first derive a design criterion that reveals the brute-force demodulation complexity depends on the first derivative of the phase function. Then, we introduce a family of phase functions that can increase the brute-force demodulation complexity in an unbounded and controllable manner, while preserving chirp structure of AFDM. Our simulation results demonstrate that the proposed phase function design enhances the PLS performance of AFDM by several orders of magnitude compared with the conventional AFDM in terms of brute-force demodulation complexity.

中文摘要翻译: 仿射频分复用（AFDM）近期因其对多普勒效应的鲁棒性以及在集成感知与通信（ISAC）中的优势，成为一种有前景的高机动通信波形。AFDM利用具有两个可调参数的啁啾子载波调制发送数据符号：一个参数用于应对多普勒效应，第二个参数可用于物理层安全（PLS）。本文聚焦于将第二个啁啾参数设计为通用相位函数的形式，以增强波形对窃听者暴力解调的鲁棒性。具体而言，我们首先推导了一个设计准则，揭示了暴力解调复杂度取决于相位函数的一阶导数。随后，我们引入了一类相位函数，能够在保持AFDM啁啾结构的同时，以无界且可控的方式提升暴力解调复杂度。仿真结果表明，在暴力解调复杂度方面，所提出的相位函数设计相比传统AFDM，将AFDM的物理层安全性能提升了数个数量级。

</details>


### [112] [An integration-free approach for particle flow filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14852)
*Domonkos Csuzdi,Tamás Bécsi,Olivér Törő*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了粒子流滤波器在矢量线性高斯测量下的精确闭式解，避免了数值积分，等价于卡尔曼测量更新，并通过N步切片法缓解了非线性模型中的刚度问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统对数同宿粒子流滤波器需要数值积分，计算成本高且易受刚度问题影响，亟需一种无需数值积分的精确解析解。

Method: 将控制粒子迁移的伪时间常微分方程变换到特定特征空间，推导出齐次状态转移矩阵和非齐次强迫项的闭式代数表达式，得到精确Daum-Huang确定性粒子流的解析解；进而将该闭式计算嵌入N步切片方法中，推广到高度非线性测量模型。

Result: 理论证明该解析解在数学上等价于精确的卡尔曼测量更新，并通过N步切片法有效缓解了非线性模型中的刚度问题，实现了无需积分的粒子更新。

Conclusion: 所提出的闭式解方法在保持精度的同时显著降低了计算负担，为非线性贝叶斯估计提供了一种高效、无刚度的替代方案。

Original Abstract: Log-homotopy particle flow filters realize nonlinear Bayesian estimation by continuously migrating samples from the prior to the posterior distribution. This transport is governed by a pseudo-time ordinary differential equation (ODE). A major practical challenge of these filters is the need for numerical integration, which suffers from high computational cost and susceptibility to stiffness. This paper develops an exact, integration-free closed-form solution for the exact Daum--Huang (EDH) deterministic particle flow under vector linear Gaussian measurements. By transforming the ODE into a specific eigenspace, closed-form algebraic expressions are derived for both the homogeneous state transition matrix and the inhomogeneous forcing term. We prove that this analytic solution is mathematically equivalent to the exact Kalman measurement update. Furthermore, we demonstrate how this closed-form evaluation can be embedded within an $N$-step slicing method, providing a stiffness-mitigating, integration-free particle update for highly nonlinear measurement models.

中文摘要翻译: 对数同宿粒子流滤波器通过将样本从先验分布连续迁移到后验分布来实现非线性贝叶斯估计。这种迁移由一个伪时间常微分方程（ODE）控制。这些滤波器的一个主要实际挑战是需要数值积分，而数值积分存在计算成本高且易受刚度影响的问题。本文针对矢量线性高斯测量下的精确Daum-Huang（EDH）确定性粒子流，提出了一种精确的、无需积分的闭式解。通过将ODE变换到特定特征空间，推导出了齐次状态转移矩阵和非齐次强迫项的闭式代数表达式。我们证明该解析解在数学上等价于精确的卡尔曼测量更新。此外，我们展示了如何将该闭式计算嵌入到N步切片方法中，为高度非线性的测量模型提供一种缓解刚度、无需积分的粒子更新方式。

</details>


### [113] [Transmit Beamforming for High-Rate Underwater Acoustic Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14919)
*Diego A. Cuji,Andrew C. Singer,Milica Stojanovic*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对水声通信中发射波束成形对信道状态信息高度依赖的问题，提出一种基于角度（主导路径）的波束成形策略，利用传播场中相对稳定的几何结构成分来减少对完整信道信息的依赖，并在SPACE和MACE数据集上验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统水声通信发射波束成形需要预先精确掌握接收端信道信息，但实际中信道估计通过反馈获得，由于反馈延迟和信道变化，估计结果往往带有噪声或已过时，导致性能下降。因此需要一种对完整信道信息依赖更低的波束成形方法。

Method: 提出一种基于角度（angle-based）的发射波束成形策略，利用传播场中几何结构中的稳定成分（尤其是随时间保持相对稳定的主导路径），通过提取该主导路径的角度信息进行波束成形，从而减少对完整信道状态信息的依赖。

Result: 在SPACE和MACE数据集上的实验结果表明，所提方法在数据检测均方误差（MSE）和误码率（BER）方面均表现出有效性。

Conclusion: 基于角度（主导路径）的发射波束成形策略能够有效降低对完整信道信息的依赖，在水声通信中具有实际应用价值。

Original Abstract: Transmit beamforming for underwater acoustic communication is challenging because it requires perfect knowledge of the channel to the receiver in advance. In practice, channel estimates must be learned through feedback and are often noisy or outdated because of feedback delay and channel variation. In this paper, we investigate angle-based beamforming strategies for a single-user link that reduce dependence on full channel knowledge by exploiting stable components of the geometric structure in the propagation field. In particular, we focus on scenarios in which there exists a dominant path that remains relatively stable over time, making it a suitable candidate for transmit beamforming. Experimental results using the SPACE and MACE data sets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method in terms of data-detection mean-squared error and bit error rate.

中文摘要翻译: 水声通信中的发射波束成形具有挑战性，因为它需要预先精确知道接收端的信道信息。在实践中，信道估计必须通过反馈学习获得，且由于反馈延迟和信道变化，估计结果往往带有噪声或已过时。本文研究面向单用户链路的基于角度（angle-based）的波束成形策略，通过利用传播场中几何结构的稳定成分来减少对完整信道信息的依赖。具体而言，我们聚焦于存在随时间保持相对稳定的主导路径的场景，该路径是发射波束成形的合适候选。使用SPACE和MACE数据集的实验结果证明了所提方法在数据检测均方误差和误码率方面的有效性。

</details>


### [114] [nASR: An End-to-End Trainable Neural Layer for Channel-Level EEG Artifact Subspace Reconstruction in Real-Time BCI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14941)
*Shantanu Sarkar,Jose L. Contreras-Vidal*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: nASR是一种端到端可训练的Keras层，通过引入两个可训练阈值参数（K和L）改进传统伪影子空间重建（ASR），在BCI应用中实现更优的伪影去除与解码性能，同时推理时间减少6-8倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统ASR在EEG信号伪影滤波中性能严重依赖阈值参数，错误阈值可能去除任务相关神经特征；此外，在主成分空间进行子空间重建可能改变底层数据结构，丢弃关键神经信息。

Method: 提出nASR，一种端到端可训练的Keras层，联合优化伪影抑制与下游解码。引入两个可训练阈值参数：K控制PC方差空间中的伪影检测，L量化特征值分散度以定位主伪影贡献通道，实现选择性通道级重建以保留干净通道信息。

Result: 在BCI Competition IV Dataset 1的两个受试者上，五种nASR变体（m01-m05）的消融实验证实nASR在分类指标上一致优于传统ASR，同时推理时间减少6-8倍。

Conclusion: nASR是面向低延迟和高解码性能的实时BCI应用的强有力候选方案。

Original Abstract: Electroencephalogram (EEG) signals are highly susceptible to artifacts, resulting in a low signal-to-noise ratio which makes extraction of meaningful neural information challenging. Artifact Subspace Reconstruction (ASR) is one of the most widely used artifact filtering techniques in EEG-based BCI applications, owing to its real-time applicability. ASR reconstructs artifact-free signals by operating in Principal Component (PC) space within sliding windows. However, ASR performance is critically sensitive to its threshold parameter - an incorrect threshold risks removing task-relevant neural features alongside artifacts. Furthermore, since PCs are linear combinations of all channels, subspace reconstruction in PC space may alter the underlying data structure, potentially discarding essential neural information.
  To address these limitations, we propose nASR, a novel end-to-end trainable Keras layer that jointly optimizes artifact rejection and downstream decoding. nASR introduces two trainable threshold parameters: K, which governs artifact detection in PC variance space, and L, which quantifies eigen-spread to pinpoint the primary artifact--contributing channels, enabling selective channel-level reconstruction that preserves clean channel information.
  An ablation study comprising five model variants (m01 - m05), evaluated across two subjects from the BCI Competition IV Dataset 1, confirms that nASR variants consistently outperform traditional ASR on test classification metrics, while achieving a 6-8x reduction in inference time, making nASR a strong candidate for real-time BCI applications demanding both low latency and high decoding performance.

中文摘要翻译: 脑电图（EEG）信号极易受到伪影的影响，导致信噪比低，使得提取有意义的神经信息面临挑战。伪影子空间重建（ASR）因其实时适用性，是基于EEG的脑机接口（BCI）应用中最广泛使用的伪影滤波技术之一。ASR通过在滑动窗口内对主成分（PC）空间进行操作来重建无伪影信号。然而，ASR的性能对其阈值参数极为敏感——错误的阈值可能会将任务相关的神经特征与伪影一同去除。此外，由于主成分是所有通道的线性组合，PC空间中的子空间重建可能会改变底层数据结构，从而可能丢弃关键的神经信息。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了nASR，一种新颖的端到端可训练的Keras层，它能够联合优化伪影抑制与下游解码任务。nASR引入了两个可训练阈值参数：K，用于控制PC方差空间中的伪影检测；L，用于量化特征值分散度以定位主要的伪影贡献通道，从而实现选择性的通道级重建，保留干净通道的信息。一项包含五种模型变体（m01-m05）的消融研究，在BCI Competition IV Dataset 1的两个受试者上进行评估，证实了nASR变体在测试分类指标上一致优于传统ASR，同时推理时间减少了6-8倍，这使得nASR成为对低延迟和高解码性能均有要求的实时BCI应用的有力候选方案。

</details>


### [115] [Map2APS: A Physically Grounded Benchmark for Direct Angle Power Spectrum Prediction from Urban Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14989)
*Junxi Huang,Xiucheng Wang,Nan Cheng,Kailong Wang,Ruijin Sun,Zhisheng Yin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了Map2APS，一个基于智能射线追踪构建的大规模物理基准数据集，用于从环境地图直接预测角度功率谱，并提出了MS-AReg基线模型，在跨地图泛化任务上取得了优异性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有环境感知学习主要关注无线电地图和路径损耗预测，但缺乏从地图直接预测角度功率谱（APS）的标准大规模基准数据集，而APS对波束管理和MIMO处理至关重要。

Method: 利用智能射线追踪（IRT）构建包含51个城市地图和约255万收发样本的Map2APS基准数据集，采用严格跨地图划分评估泛化能力，并提出MS-AReg作为强参考基线模型。

Result: 在249,993个完整保留测试样本上，MS-AReg达到0.948的余弦相似度、1.20°的峰值位置误差和0.101 ms/样本的推理延迟；同时报告了Top-1主峰命中率和主峰召回率等主方向指标。

Conclusion: Map2APS为地图到APS预测提供了标准化物理基准，MS-AReg基线证明了方法的有效性，相关基准、代码和评估脚本已开源。

Original Abstract: Angle power spectrum (APS) characterizes the directional distribution of received signal power and is directly relevant to beam management and MIMO processing. While environment-aware learning has been widely studied for radio maps and path loss, direct map-to-APS prediction still lacks a standardized large-scale benchmark. This paper presents Map2APS, a physically grounded benchmark constructed from intelligent ray-tracing (IRT) path-level propagation records. Map2APS covers 51 equal-height urban maps and approximately 2.55 million Tx--Rx samples, with a strict cross-map split for evaluating generalization to unseen urban layouts. We benchmark representative model families and introduce MS-AReg as a strong reference baseline. On the full held-out test set of 249{,}993 samples, MS-AReg achieves a cosine similarity of 0.948, a peak location error of 1.20$^\circ$, and an inference latency of 0.101 ms/sample. We further report dominant-direction metrics, including Top-1 dominant peak hit rate and dominant peak recall, to evaluate whether predicted spectra preserve decision-relevant arrival directions. The benchmark, code, and evaluation scripts are released at https://github.com/UNIC-Lab/aps-data.

中文摘要翻译: 角度功率谱（APS）描述了接收信号功率的方向分布，与波束管理和MIMO处理直接相关。虽然环境感知学习已被广泛研究用于无线电地图和路径损耗预测，但直接从地图到APS的预测仍缺乏标准化的大规模基准。本文提出了Map2APS，一个基于智能射线追踪（IRT）路径级传播记录构建的物理基准。Map2APS涵盖51个等高度城市地图和约255万个收发样本，并采用严格的跨地图划分来评估对未见城市布局的泛化能力。我们对代表性模型族进行了基准测试，并引入MS-AReg作为强参考基线。在包含249,993个样本的完整保留测试集上，MS-AReg达到了0.948的余弦相似度、1.20°的峰值位置误差和0.101 ms/样本的推理延迟。我们进一步报告了主方向指标，包括Top-1主峰命中率和主峰召回率，以评估预测谱是否保留了决策相关的到达方向。该基准、代码和评估脚本已在https://github.com/UNIC-Lab/aps-data开源。

</details>


### [116] [Downlink Performance Analysis of Pinching Antenna Systems: WDMA or NOMA?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15129)
*Han Zhang,Bingxin Zhang,Yizhe Zhao,Kun Yang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文对采用波导分多址（WDMA）和非正交多址（NOMA）的下行链路pinching天线系统（PASS）进行了分析，建立了统一信道模型，推导了中断概率和平均可达速率的闭式与单积分表达式，并通过蒙特卡洛仿真验证。结果表明：NOMA在高信噪比下因连续干扰消除（SIC）获得更高频谱效率，WDMA在低中SNR下更可靠但存在中断地板和速率饱和，且WDMA性能对用户空间分布更敏感。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 为pinching天线系统（PASS）中的多址接入方案（WDMA与NOMA）提供统一的性能分析框架，揭示两种方案在不同信噪比和用户空间分布条件下的性能差异，为接入方案选择和天线部署提供设计指导。

Method: 建立统一信道模型，涵盖天线部署、用户空间分布和路径损耗；推导中断概率和平均可达速率的闭式表达式与单积分表达式；采用蒙特卡洛仿真进行验证。

Result: NOMA在高发射信噪比下因连续干扰消除（SIC）实现更高频谱效率；WDMA在低至中SNR下性能更可靠，但在高SNR下存在中断地板和速率饱和现象；WDMA性能对用户空间分布更为敏感，原因是空间依赖的波导间干扰。

Conclusion: 本文为PASS系统中接入方案的选择和天线放置提供了设计见解：高SNR场景宜选择NOMA以获取高谱效，低中SNR场景宜选择WDMA以保证可靠性，同时WDMA部署需更关注用户空间分布的影响。

Original Abstract: This paper presents an analytical framework for downlink pinching antenna systems (PASS) employing waveguide division multiple access (WDMA) and non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA). A unified channel model is developed to capture antenna deployment, user spatial distribution, and path loss. Closed-form and single-integral expressions for the outage probability and average achievable rate are derived and validated via Monte Carlo simulations. The results show that NOMA achieves higher spectral efficiency at high transmit signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) due to successive interference cancellation (SIC), whereas WDMA offers more reliable performance at low to moderate SNR but suffers from an outage floor and rate saturation at high SNR. Moreover, WDMA performance is more sensitive to the user spatial distribution due to the spatially dependent inter-waveguide interference. These findings provide design insights for access-scheme selection and antenna placement in PASS.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一个适用于下行链路pinching天线系统（PASS）的分析框架，该框架同时采用波导分多址（WDMA）和非正交多址（NOMA）。我们建立了一个统一信道模型，以捕获天线部署、用户空间分布和路径损耗的影响。推导了中断概率和平均可达速率的闭式表达式及单积分表达式，并通过蒙特卡洛仿真进行了验证。结果表明：在高发射信噪比（SNR）下，NOMA因连续干扰消除（SIC）能够实现更高的频谱效率；而WDMA在低至中SNR下提供更可靠的性能，但在高SNR下存在中断地板和速率饱和现象。此外，由于空间依赖的波导间干扰，WDMA性能对用户空间分布更为敏感。这些发现为PASS中的接入方案选择和天线放置提供了设计见解。

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Vision-Based Runtime Monitoring under Varying Specifications using Semantic Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13923)
*Bardh Hoxha,Oliver Schön,Hideki Okamoto,Lars Lindemann,Georgios Fainekos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对部分可观测条件下基于视觉观测的过去时间信号时序逻辑（ptSTL）的认证运行时监控问题，提出了一种可复用的监控框架。通过语义基（semantic basis）作为最小预测目标，结合单调1-Lipschitz可复用接口，实现单次共形校准即可认证整个公式片段，无需逐公式重新训练。同时引入滚动预测监控器（rolling prediction monitor），仅预测当前谓词值并在线重构时序历史。在行人交叉路口基准和Waymo实际驾驶数据上验证了方法的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的运行时监控方法通常需要针对每个时序逻辑公式单独训练和校准，缺乏可复用性，导致计算成本高昂。本文旨在设计一种可重用的监控接口，使得一次训练和校准后能够认证目标片段中的任意公式，同时提供有限样本保证。

Method: 1) 提出语义基（semantic basis）概念，即时序原子鲁棒性分数向量，作为单调1-Lipschitz可复用接口类中的最小预测目标；2) 基于解析树的确定性解码器评估任意公式；3) 单次共形校准即可认证整个公式片段，无需联合界（union bound）；4) 引入滚动预测监控器，仅预测当前谓词值并通过在线方式重构时序历史。

Result: 在行人交叉路口基准上，滚动监控器在短时间范围内得到更紧的认证界，而语义基监控器在长时间范围内紧度提升高达4倍。在Waymo实际驾驶数据上，两种监控器均经验性地满足了共形覆盖保证。

Conclusion: 所提出的可复用监控框架通过语义基实现了高效的认证运行时监控，能够在无需逐公式重新训练的情况下提供有限样本保证。滚动监控器与语义基监控器在不同时间尺度下各有优势，实际数据验证了方法的有效性。

Original Abstract: We study certified runtime monitoring of past-time signal temporal logic (ptSTL) from visual observations under partial observability. The monitor must infer safety-relevant quantities from images and provide finite-sample guarantees, while being \emph{reusable}: once trained and calibrated, it should certify any formula in a target fragment without per-formula retraining. For fragments induced by a finite dictionary of temporal atoms, we prove that the \emph{semantic basis}, the vector of atom robustness scores, is the minimum prediction target within the class of monotone, 1-Lipschitz reusable interfaces: any formula is evaluated by a deterministic decoder derived from the parse tree, and a single conformal calibration pass certifies the entire fragment with no union bound. We also introduce a \emph{rolling prediction monitor} that predicts only current predicate values and reconstructs temporal history online; this is easier to learn but grows conservative at long horizons. On a pedestrian-crossroad benchmark, rolling achieves tighter certified bounds at short horizons while the semantic-basis monitor is up to 4-times tighter at long horizons. We validate the presented monitors on real-world Waymo driving data, where both monitors satisfy the conformal coverage guarantee empirically.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了在部分可观测条件下，基于视觉观测对过去时间信号时序逻辑（ptSTL）进行认证运行时监控的问题。监控器必须从图像中推断与安全相关的量并提供有限样本保证，同时具备可复用性：一旦训练并校准完成，它应能认证目标片段中的任意公式，而无需逐公式重新训练。对于由有限时序原子词典诱导的公式片段，我们证明语义基（即原子鲁棒性分数向量）是单调1-Lipschitz可复用接口类中的最小预测目标：任意公式均可通过由解析树导出的确定性解码器进行评估，且单次共形校准即可认证整个片段，无需联合界。我们还引入了一种滚动预测监控器，它仅预测当前谓词值并在线重构时序历史；这种方法更易于学习，但在长时间范围内会变得保守。在行人交叉路口基准上，滚动监控器在短时间范围内获得了更紧的认证界，而语义基监控器在长时间范围内的紧度提升高达4倍。我们在真实世界的Waymo驾驶数据上验证了所提出的监控器，两种监控器均经验性地满足了共形覆盖保证。

</details>


### [118] [Mechanistic Interpretability of EEG Foundation Models via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13930)
*William Lehn-Schiøler,Magnus Ruud Kjær,Rahul Thapa,Magnus Guldberg Pedersen,Anton Storgaard Mosquera,Nick Williams,Radu Gatej,Tue Lehn-Schiøler,Sándor Beniczky,Sadasivan Puthusserypady,James Zou,Lars Kai Hansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用TopK稀疏自编码器（SAE）从三种不同架构的EEG基础模型（SleepFM、REVE、LaBraM）中提取稀疏特征字典，通过临床分类体系评估特征的单语义性与纠缠性，并利用概念引导揭示模型内部表征的失败模式（如“破球”干预和临床纠缠），最后通过频谱解码器将潜在操作映射为可解释的生理频率特征。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: EEG基础模型在临床任务中表现优异，但其内部计算机制不透明，阻碍了临床信任。现有方法缺乏对模型内部特征可解释性的系统评估框架。

Method: 1）在三种架构的EEG Transformer（SleepFM、REVE、LaBraM）上应用TopK稀疏自编码器提取稀疏特征字典；2）基于临床分类体系（异常、年龄、性别、用药）对特征进行锚定，评估单语义性与纠缠性；3）通过单一超参数程序（基于字典健康审计）实现跨架构迁移；4）提出“目标vs非目标探针区域”指标量化概念引导的选择性，揭示三种操作状态；5）使用频谱解码器将引导操作映射回幅度谱。

Result: 1）单一超参数程序可鲁棒地迁移至三种架构；2）发现三种操作状态：可选择引导、已编码但纠缠、未编码；3）揭示关键表征失败模式：“破球”干预会破坏全局模型性能，以及年龄-病理混杂等临床纠缠现象；4）成功将潜在操作映射为病理慢波抑制和α频带恢复等可解释的生理频率特征。

Conclusion: 该框架系统揭示了EEG基础模型内部表征的局限性与纠缠性，为提升模型可解释性和临床可信度提供了分析工具，并为未来EEG模型的可解释性研究奠定了基础。

Original Abstract: EEG foundation models achieve state-of-the-art clinical performance, yet the internal computations driving their predictions remain opaque: a barrier to clinical trust. We apply TopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) across three architecturally distinct EEG transformers: SleepFM, REVE, and LaBraM to extract sparse feature dictionaries from their embeddings. By grounding these features in a clinical taxonomy (abnormality, age, sex, and medication), we benchmark monosemanticity and entanglement across architectures. A single hyperparameter procedure, driven by an intrinsic dictionary health audit, transfers robustly across all three architectures. Via concept steering, we introduce a "target vs. off-target" probe area metric to quantify steering selectivity and reveal three operational regimes: selectively steerable, encoded but entangled, and non-encoded. This framework exposes critical representational failures: "wrecking-ball" interventions that collapse global model performance, and clinical entanglements, such as age-pathology confounding, where it is impossible to suppress one concept without corrupting the other. Finally, a spectral decoder maps these interventions back to the amplitude spectrum, translating latent manipulations into physiologically interpretable frequency signatures, such as pathological slow-wave suppression and $α$-band restoration.

中文摘要翻译: EEG基础模型在临床任务中达到了最先进的性能，但其驱动预测的内部计算仍然不透明——这是阻碍临床信任的障碍。我们在三种架构不同的EEG Transformer模型（SleepFM、REVE和LaBraM）上应用TopK稀疏自编码器（SAE），从其嵌入中提取稀疏特征字典。通过将这些特征锚定到临床分类体系（异常、年龄、性别和用药），我们跨架构基准测试了单语义性和纠缠性。一个由内在字典健康审计驱动的单一超参数程序，能够鲁棒地迁移至所有三种架构。通过概念引导，我们引入了一种“目标vs非目标探针区域”指标来量化引导选择性，并揭示了三种操作状态：可选择引导的、已编码但纠缠的、以及未编码的。该框架暴露了关键的表征失败模式：“破球”式干预会破坏全局模型性能，以及临床纠缠现象（如年龄-病理混杂），其中抑制一个概念而不破坏另一个概念是不可能的。最后，频谱解码器将这些干预映射回幅度谱，将潜在操作转化为生理上可解释的频率特征，例如病理性慢波抑制和α频带恢复。

</details>


### [119] [REALM: Retrospective Encoder Alignment for LFP Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14867)
*Peicheng Wu,Zhenyu Bu,Runze Ma,Lin Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REALM框架，通过回顾性蒸馏将非因果LFP解码模型转化为因果模型，实现实时神经解码，性能优于现有方法，参数量减少2倍，训练时间减少10倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尖峰信号的高采样率成为高通道无线BCI的带宽和功耗瓶颈；LFP虽具有稳定性好、能耗低等优点，但基于LFP的解码模型精度较低且多采用不适合实时部署的非因果架构。需弥合离线高性能模型与实时因果模型之间的差距。

Method: 提出REALM框架，首先预训练一个双向Mamba-2教师模型（掩码自编码目标），然后通过表示对齐和任务监督的联合目标，将教师模型蒸馏为一个紧凑的学生因果模型，实现离线到在线的知识迁移。

Result: REALM在行为解码任务上一致优于因果和非因果的LFP基线方法；参数量减少2倍，训练时间减少10倍；证明仅基于LFP的模型无需依赖尖峰信号即可达到竞争性解码性能。

Conclusion: 回顾性蒸馏有效弥合了离线神经解码与实时神经解码之间的差距；REALM为下一代无线可植入BCI提供了实用且可扩展的LFP-only替代方案。

Original Abstract: Spike activity has been the dominant neural signal for behavior decoding due to its high spatial and temporal resolution. However, as brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) move toward high channel counts and wireless operation, the high sampling frequency of spike signals becomes a bottleneck due to high power and bandwidth requirements. Local field potentials (LFPs) represent a different spatial-temporal scale of brain activity compared to spikes, offering key advantages including improved long-term stability, reduced energy consumption, and lower bandwidth requirement. Despite these benefits, LFP-based decoding models typically show reduced accuracy and often rely on non-causal architectures that are unsuitable for real-time deployment. To address these challenges, we propose REALM: a retrospective distillation framework that enables causal LFP decoding. Inspired by offline-to-online distillation strategies in speech recognition, REALM transfers representational knowledge from a pretrained multi-session bidirectional LFP model to a causal version for real-time deployment. We first pretrain a bidirectional Mamba-2 teacher model using a masked autoencoding objective. We then distill this teacher model into a compact student model via a combined objective of representation alignment and task supervision. REALM consistently outperforms both causal and non-causal LFP-based SOTA methods for behavior decoding. Notably, our REALM improves decoding performance while achieving a $2\times$ reduction in parameter count and a $10\times$ reduction in training time. These results demonstrate that retrospective distillation effectively bridges the gap between offline and real-time neural decoding. REALM shows that LFP-only models can achieve competitive decoding performance without reliance on spike signals, offering a practical and scalable alternative for next-generation wireless implantable BCIs.
